<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Phantom_Thief">Phantom Thief</span></h2>
<table class="sortable mw-collapsible mw-collapsed fandom-table">
<caption>
</caption>
<tbody><tr>
<th>Class Name
</th>
<th>Ability
</th>
<th>AP
</th>
<th>Priority
</th>
<th>Stat Additives
</th>
<th>Skill Definition
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Soul Deceiver
</td>
<td>False Relapse
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>50
</td>
<td>+15 Cunning, +5 Dexterity, +3 Intelligence
</td>
<td>An enhanced base mechanic. The Phantom Thief learns how to use downtime to their advantage without leaving openings. <b>Downtime can now be activated at any time, even if the opponent is attacking or in the middle of an action.</b> Once activating downtime, the Phantom Thief will effectivelly cloak their bodily movements and drop "dead", while in this action the Phantom Thief is considered aware. The Phantom Thief will be in passive downtime in this state, reaping twice the benefits. If attacked/interupted, the Nimble Assassin will swiftly arise and cancel the opposing action, forcing a bonus turn of their own.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>True Trickery
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>100
</td>
<td>+10 Cunning, +20 WM
</td>
<td>Deception is the key part of being a phantom in disguise. When choosing to attack anyone with a physical attack, the Phantom Thief can enable their ability to phase through their targets and redirect attacks back at the target, performing a fake-out. The attack negates previous rolls against defensive actions (Countering/Counter-Attacks). The Phantom Thief's attack capabilities can be chosen to stay the same or redone. The attack is stackable.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Phantom Assassin
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>60
</td>
<td>+20 Cunning, +10 Explosive Movement, +5 Dexterity
</td>
<td>Thief or Assassin, the Phantom has the ability the phase through others. When this action is done, the Phantom Thief has the ability to take the weapon or held items from others. Soul-Bounded items will still be taken. The Phantom Thief gains the ability to use these weapons and attacks utilizing the stolen aspects from others.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Phase Shift
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>80
</td>
<td>+50 Cunning
</td>
<td>The key to being a Phantom and Thief is the key ability to trick and compromise. The Phantom Thief can phase through concrete matter such as grounds and walls temporarily disappearing from the battle, appear beneath a target while still phased and attack while unnoticed. The attack can be strangulation to the ground, neck slice, or anything that enables actions. All attacks will gain a 2d15 Suffocation Damage, 1d40 Crushing Damage, and take 25% from bleed damage.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Disregarding Fake-out
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>-
</td>
<td>+40 Action Priority, +40 Priority
</td>
<td>The Phantom Thief is the master of the dagger and abilities revolving around its physical self. All rolls considering with positive or negative actions will all be skewed and pertained to a positive view. "Critical Failures" are no longer existent with physical actions, instead this will be changed to failures.  "Critical Successes" will be skewed down to included a new type, "Ultimate Successes" which will deal 4x the original damage and include 5d12 True Damage.
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Kings_Hand">Kings Hand</span></h2>
<table class="sortable mw-collapsible mw-collapsed fandom-table">
<caption>
</caption>
<tbody><tr>
<th>Class Name
</th>
<th>Ability
</th>
<th>AP
</th>
<th>Priority
</th>
<th>Stat Additives
</th>
<th>Skill Definition
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Soul of Millions
</td>
<td>Relapse of Time
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>1
</td>
<td>+15 Cunning, +5 Dexterity, +3 Intelligence
</td>
<td>During your Downtime or when another creature within 50 feet of you takes an Opportunity Action, you can conjure a weapon using neutral energy. This weapon must be one you have seen or held at least once during the entire Stage (Campaign).
<p>The conjured weapon deals 1.5x less damage than the original. However, upon summoning it, you can act immediately—even interrupting a True Opportunity Action. If you do, you will become winded immediately after the attack, causing you to lose your next turn.
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Call of Kings
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>7
</td>
<td>+10 intelligence, +20 WM
</td>
<td>During your Downtime or when another creature within 50 feet of you takes an Opportunity Action, each weapon you have a Memory of possesses a Champion—or more precisely, a King. You can enter a Mental Space, which, in-game, lasts for 2 seconds of Passive Time but can last indefinitely out of game.
<p>Within this space, you may challenge the King of the Weapon to claim its true rights. If you win, the weapon's damage is doubled, and you gain the ability to summon multiple copies of it at any time. If you lose, you permanently lose access to the weapon and forfeit 5% of your total health pool, affecting both your Defensive Hit Points and standard Health Points.
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Prodigy
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>100
</td>
<td>+20 intelligence, +10 Explosive Movement, +30 Control
</td>
<td>During your Downtime or when another creature within 100 feet of you takes an Opportunity Action, any creature wielding a weapon in any form will always deal half damage to you.
<p>If you take damage and your health falls below half, you can attune yourself to the weapon, rolling a d10 for each incoming attack. Subtract the highest roll from the damage taken, and any instance where you fully nullify damage becomes permanently nullified up to a total of 10 instances.
</p><p>Once you reach 10, you begin rolling a d20, progressing up to a d100. After reaching 100, you must battle and conquer the King of the Weapon. If you fail, you immediately take all the nullified damage at once and lose this class, requiring you to obtain it again. If you succeed, the total nullified damage is divided by 2 and converted into defensive hit points.
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Shifting Hand

<td>
</td>

<td>
</td>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>

<td>
</td>
<td>-
</td>

</td>
<td>During your Downtime or when another creature within 50 feet of you takes an Opportunity Action, all weapons you wield—if you have conquered their Kings—grant you the ability to summon them simultaneously. When you do, any creature that controls a weapon will find its effectiveness reduced by half if that weapon exists within your Archive.
<p>If the wielder's Control stat is lower than yours, their weapon will rust and disintegrate, empowering you by increasing your level by 2. However, if their Control stat is higher than yours, the weapon's King will awaken once more, and you will lose possession of the weapon. Both of you must then roll for Control. If you fail, you will also lose a level.
</p>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Honing_Striker">Honing Striker</span></h2>
<table class="sortable mw-collapsible mw-collapsed fandom-table">
<caption>
</caption>
<tbody><tr>
<th>Class Name
</th>
<th>Ability
</th>
<th>AP
</th>
<th>Priority
</th>
<th>Stat Additives
</th>
<th>Skill Definition
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Visionseeker
</td>
<td>Decaying Mark
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>35
</td>
<td>+10 Dexterity, +15 Explosive Moment
</td>
<td>The Honing Striker has perfected their ability to read their target, specializing in utilizing critical attacks to their maximum potential. Upon landing a critical a Decaying Mark will generate on the target, the mark will act as a marker providing information to the Honing Striker by showing the location at all times. The mark will have a life span of 5 rounds, after each round the target will receive 1d12 true damage. The Honing Striker can force a decay of the mark in order to instantly teleport to the target. If attacking a marked without forcing a decaying, the attack will be a guaranteed critical attack.
<p><br>
</p><p>Placing a mark via critical attack has a short cooldown of once per downtime.
</p><p>Alternate: Consume a full turn to place a mark on a target.
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Concentration Stacks
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>50
</td>
<td>+30 Precision, +20 Adaptation, +5 Intelligence
</td>
<td>Someone who has been perfecting critical strikes, the Honing Striker has became perfection at the finest, giving buffs towards critical success stacks. Each successful critical attack will grant +1d12 true damage reaching a max of +3d12 true damage <b>per turn</b> and will grant the user a bonus turn. The Honing Striker at max stacks can't progress anymore and reach a stage of clarity where if anything interrupts or forces a failure of action, the Honing Striker will face a harsh winded losing their next turn and current turn. The Honing Striker believes in the true art of perfection, if you roll anything below a 50% margin aka a failure, the attack will become instantly a critical failure with the Honing Striker receiving a harsh winded and damage around 50% of the original damage.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Fatal Clarity
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>-
</td>
<td>+10 Endurance, +10 Breath Control
</td>
<td>As a Honing Striker, utilizing opportunity turns takes a harsh toll on your body generating heavy amounts of fatigue. The Honing Striker through harsh training and control has gained the ability to partially reduce the downsides of using extra turns. Receive -1 Fatigue per extra turn.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Perfect Opportunity
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>100
</td>
<td>+10 Action Priority, +20 Priority
</td>
<td>The Honing Striker has honed their strikes to their specialty, they gain the option to choose an additional 2 numbers from failure rates to change into a critical success (NAT 20), E.I. 1-10 with 4,6. If these special 2 numbers are landed on through rolls, gain a opportunity attack that will guarantee a critical success. Afterwards your character will be harsh winded losing their next turn.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>
</td>
<td>Airbound Oscillation
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>25
</td>
<td>+15 WM, +10 BC, +15 Precision, +20 Explosive Movement
</td>
<td>To the air or to the ground, the Honing Striker is the master of precision as well as movement. Knock a target up into the air that currently has a mark placed, begin to jump up into the air and dash at the target. Use a mark to teleport to the side and dash again, do this to dash all around the target striking them 5 times total ending with a slam down. If rolling a 15+(From 20 roll) with any of the attacks, gain an opportunity attack. The slam will deal 1d10 Force Damage, if rolling a 8 or above deal 1d40 Crushing Damage.
<p><br>
Move has a cooldown of once per 3 downtimes.
</p><p>Note: All abilities in Honing Strikers are base rolls DC and not modified.
</p>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Mid-Range_Artillery:">Mid-Range Artillery:</span></h2>
<h3><span id="The_Mid-Range_Artillery_class_focuses_on_versatile_combat_capabilities_at_medium_distances._They_are_adept_at_combining_ranged_attacks_with_tactical_positioning_and_area-of-effect_abilities._These_combatants_bring_a_balanced_approach.2C_utilizing_firearms.2C_advanced_technology.2C_or_elemental_powers_to_dominate_the_battlefield."></span><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Mid-Range_Artillery_class_focuses_on_versatile_combat_capabilities_at_medium_distances._They_are_adept_at_combining_ranged_attacks_with_tactical_positioning_and_area-of-effect_abilities._These_combatants_bring_a_balanced_approach,_utilizing_firearms,_advanced_technology,_or_elemental_powers_to_dominate_the_battlefield.">The Mid-Range Artillery class focuses on versatile combat capabilities at medium distances. They are adept at combining ranged attacks with tactical positioning and area-of-effect abilities. These combatants bring a balanced approach, utilizing firearms, advanced technology, or elemental powers to dominate the battlefield.</span></h3>

<h3><span id="The_Team_Guardian_class_specializes_in_providing_protective_support_to_their_allies.2C_shielding_them_from_harm.2C_and_bolstering_their_defenses._They_are_selfless_protectors.2C_utilizing_defensive_techniques.2C_barriers.2C_healing_abilities.2C_and_protective_spells_to_ensure_the_survival_and_success_of_their_team."></span><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Team_Guardian_class_specializes_in_providing_protective_support_to_their_allies,_shielding_them_from_harm,_and_bolstering_their_defenses._They_are_selfless_protectors,_utilizing_defensive_techniques,_barriers,_healing_abilities,_and_protective_spells_to_ensure_the_survival_and_success_of_their_team.">The Team Guardian class specializes in providing protective support to their allies, shielding them from harm, and bolstering their defenses. They are selfless protectors, utilizing defensive techniques, barriers, healing abilities, and protective spells to ensure the survival and success of their team.</span></h3>

</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Unclassified:">Unclassified:</span></h2>
<h6><span class="mw-headline" id="Freelancer:">Freelancer:</span></h6>
<pre>The Freelancer class represents individuals who operate outside traditional classifications. They are versatile, adaptable, and possess a mix of skills from different disciplines. Freelancers often specialize in unconventional tactics, bringing a unique approach to combat.
</pre>
<h6><span class="mw-headline" id="Racial_Specialist:">Racial Specialist:</span></h6>
<pre>The Racial Specialist class capitalizes on the innate strengths and unique attributes of their race. They harness racial traits, cultural knowledge, or ancestral powers, gaining an edge in combat situations. Each race may have its own distinct specialties and abilities.
</pre>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Kinba:">Kinba:</span></h2>
<pre>Cartoonist Conjurer: Illustrator of Reality
</pre>
<pre>Comic Book Creator: Inkweaver of Imagination
</pre>
<pre>Demon Tainted: Shadowborne Tormentor
</pre>
<pre>Whiteout Paladin: Celestial Guardian of Purity
</pre>
<pre>Demon Ink Warrior: Abyssal Inkblade Champion
</pre>
<pre>Cartoonistic Demon: Mirthful Fiendshaper
</pre>
<pre>Dimensional Erasure Knight: Voidborne Vanquisher of Realms
</pre>
<p><br>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cartoonist_Conjurer:_Illustrator_of_Reality">Cartoonist Conjurer: Illustrator of Reality</span></h2>
<ol><li>Sketchform Manifestation: Bring drawings to life, turning them into tangible objects or creatures.</li>
<li>Chromatic Blast: Unleash vibrant blasts of energy that manifest as colorful projectiles.</li>
<li>Comic Relief: Create comical illusions and distractions to confuse and disarm enemies.</li>
<li>Reality Warp: Distort the laws of physics within a limited area, altering the perception of reality.</li>
<li>Ink Animation: Bring static images to life, transforming them into animated allies or constructs.</li>
<li>Paper Storm: Summon a storm of animated paper projectiles, capable of slashing through opponents.</li>
<li>Canvas Portal: Paint a portal to another location, allowing for instantaneous travel or escape.</li>
<li>Cartoonish Distortion: Bend and stretch the fabric of reality, warping the environment to your advantage.</li>
<li>Sketchbook Sanctuary: Create a protective bubble of animated illustrations, shielding yourself and allies from harm.</li>
<li>Comic Convergence: Merge your physical form with a drawn representation, granting enhanced abilities and durability.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Comic_Book_Creator:_Inkweaver_of_Imagination">Comic Book Creator: Inkweaver of Imagination</span></h2>
<ol><li>Panel Manipulation: Alter the layout and composition of the battlefield by rearranging reality as if it were a comic book panel.</li>
<li>Ink Summoning: Summon creatures and characters from your comic book creations to fight alongside you.</li>
<li>Word of Power: Infuse written words with magical energy, causing them to become potent spells or enchantments.</li>
<li>Storyline Alteration: Rewrite the narrative of battle, modifying the flow of events to favor your allies and hinder enemies.</li>
<li>Ink Aura: Surround yourself with an ethereal aura that enhances your physical attributes and grants resistance to damage.</li>
<li>Graphic Burst: Unleash a concentrated burst of ink energy, causing comic book-inspired explosions upon impact.</li>
<li>Character Development: Empower your allies by imbuing them with traits and abilities inspired by legendary heroes from your comics.</li>
<li>Plot Twist: Unleash unexpected and unpredictable effects on the battlefield, catching opponents off guard.</li>
<li>Ink Shield: Create a protective force field made of ink, capable of absorbing and reflecting incoming attacks.</li>
<li>Epic Climax: Channel the essence of an epic comic book finale, unleashing a devastating and awe-inspiring ultimate attack.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Demon_Tainted:_Shadowborne_Tormentor">Demon Tainted: Shadowborne Tormentor</span></h2>
<ol><li>Abyssal Flames: Cloak yourself in dark flames, augmenting your attacks with infernal energy.</li>
<li>Tainted Visage: Invoke a horrifying demonic visage, causing fear and panic in your enemies.</li>
<li>Shadowstep: Blend into the shadows, teleporting instantly to another location with a cloud of darkness.</li>
<li>Infernal Binding: Bind opponents with ethereal chains of demonic energy, restricting their movement and abilities.</li>
<li>Cursed Touch: Infuse your strikes with dark energy, causing lingering damage and debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Soul Leech: Drain the life force of enemies, siphoning their vitality to replenish your own health.</li>
<li>Demonic Resilience: Tap into your demonic nature to enhance your physical resilience and resistance to damage.</li>
<li>Hellfire Burst: Unleash a burst of hellfire in a targeted area, causing widespread devastation and burning foes.</li>
<li>Diabolical Pact: Forge a pact with a demon, gaining temporary access to powerful demonic abilities and transformations.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Shroud yourself in a veil of darkness, becoming nearly invisible and gaining heightened stealth and agility.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Whiteout_Paladin:_Celestial_Guardian_of_Purity">Whiteout Paladin: Celestial Guardian of Purity</span></h2>
<ol><li>Radiant Smite: Channel divine energy into your weapon, smiting enemies with blinding bursts of holy light.</li>
<li>Sacred Shield: Create a shield of pure light that absorbs incoming damage and grants protection to yourself and nearby allies.</li>
<li>Purifying Aura: Emit a radiant aura that cleanses the area of corruption, dispelling curses and restoring purity.</li>
<li>Divine Intervention: Call upon celestial beings to intervene in battle, providing aid and support to you and your allies.</li>
<li>Healing Light: Channel healing energy, mending wounds and revitalizing the health of yourself and your allies.</li>
<li>Righteous Judgment: Pass judgment upon the wicked, unleashing a powerful attack that deals increased damage to evil beings.</li>
<li>Holy Resurgence: Revive fallen allies with a surge of divine energy, bringing them back into the fight with renewed vigor.</li>
<li>Aegis of Light: Manifest a shield of divine energy that blocks incoming attacks and reflects harm back to the attacker.</li>
<li>Cleansing Wave: Unleash a wave of purifying light that dispels curses, diseases, and negative effects from yourself and nearby allies.</li>
<li>Celestial Ascendance: Temporarily transform into a celestial being, radiating pure light and gaining immense power and resilience.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Demon_Ink_Warrior:_Abyssal_Inkblade_Champion">Demon Ink Warrior: Abyssal Inkblade Champion</span></h2>
<ol><li>Infernal Slash: Infuse your weapon with demonic energy, delivering devastating strikes that leave burning wounds on your enemies.</li>
<li>Ink Shield: Create a shield made of ink that absorbs incoming attacks and grants temporary protection.</li>
<li>Abyssal Mark: Imbue your weapon with a dark enchantment, causing it to drain the life force of enemies and heal yourself.</li>
<li>Shadowstep: Teleport through shadows, appearing behind your enemies to launch surprise attacks.</li>
<li>Demonic Fury: Tap into your inner demon, enhancing your physical strength and speed, allowing for lightning-fast and powerful strikes.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Blend into the shadows, becoming nearly invisible and gaining increased stealth and evasion.</li>
<li>Ink Whirlwind: Unleash a whirlwind of dark ink, slashing through enemies and leaving them vulnerable to further attacks.</li>
<li>Ebon Chains: Bind enemies with ethereal chains of ink, immobilizing them and draining their energy.</li>
<li>Abyssal Wave: Summon a wave of inky darkness that crashes over your foes, causing damage and leaving them disoriented.</li>
<li>Inkborne Berserker: Enter a state of uncontrollable rage, boosting your physical abilities and unleashing a relentless assault on your enemies.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cartoonistic_Demon:_Mirthful_Fiendshaper">Cartoonistic Demon: Mirthful Fiendshaper</span></h2>
<ol><li>Comic Mischief: Manipulate reality with cartoon-like effects, creating exaggerated and whimsical phenomena.</li>
<li>Anvil Drop: Summon an enormous cartoonish anvil that falls from the sky, crushing enemies beneath its weight.</li>
<li>Elastic Limbs: Stretch your limbs to absurd lengths, allowing for increased reach and unorthodox attacks.</li>
<li>Toon Transformation: Transform into a cartoon caricature, gaining enhanced agility and cartoon physics.</li>
<li>Banana Peel: Scatter banana peels on the ground, causing enemies to slip and lose their balance.</li>
<li>Paint Splatter: Throw paint-filled balloons that explode on impact, leaving enemies covered in vibrant hues that obstruct their vision.</li>
<li>Loony Laughter: Emit a contagious laughter that disorients enemies, causing them to lose focus and coordination.</li>
<li>Acme Arsenal: Summon an array of comically oversized and exaggerated weapons to wield in battle.</li>
<li>Reality Bending: Twist and distort the laws of physics, causing the battlefield to become a playground of absurdity.</li>
<li>Cartoon Catastrophe: Unleash a chaotic and unpredictable event, transforming the battlefield into a whimsical and chaotic cartoon world.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Dimensional_Erasure_Knight:_Voidborne_Vanquisher_of_Realms">Dimensional Erasure Knight: Voidborne Vanquisher of Realms</span></h2>
<ol><li>Rift Strike: Open a temporary dimensional rift and unleash a devastating melee strike that bypasses defenses.</li>
<li>Dimensional Shroud: Phase in and out of existence, becoming intangible and impervious to physical harm.</li>
<li>Reality Sunder: Manipulate the fabric of reality, causing fractures and instabilities in the environment that disorient enemies.</li>
<li>Void Nova: Release a surge of pure void energy, creating an explosive blast that damages and disintegrates foes.</li>
<li>Warp Step: Instantaneously teleport short distances by folding space, allowing for swift repositioning and surprise attacks.</li>
<li>Dimensional Ripper: Tear open a temporary portal to another dimension, unleashing chaotic energies upon your enemies.</li>
<li>Annihilation Blade: Infuse your weapon with unstable dimensional energy, rending through the defenses of even the most resilient foes.</li>
<li>Singularity Grasp: Create a localized gravitational anomaly that pulls enemies towards you, leaving them vulnerable to your attacks.</li>
<li>Voidwalk: Merge with the void, traversing dimensions effortlessly and gaining heightened senses and awareness.</li>
<li>Reality Collapse: Unleash the full force of dimensional energy, causing a catastrophic collapse of space and time within a specific area.</li></ol>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Sea_Lord:_Tidal_Sovereign_of_the_Deep">Sea Lord: Tidal Sovereign of the Deep</span></h2>
<ol><li>Aquatic Dominion: Command the forces of the sea, bending water to your will and summoning powerful tidal waves to devastate your enemies.</li>
<li>Trident Mastery: Wield a legendary trident with unparalleled skill, striking with precision and channeling the raw power of the ocean.</li>
<li>Poseidon's Blessing: Invoke the favor of the sea god, granting enhanced strength, agility, and resilience to yourself and nearby allies.</li>
<li>Oceanic Fury: Tap into the fury of the ocean, imbuing your attacks with crashing waves and surges of water that batter and drench your foes.</li>
<li>Sea's Embrace: Merge with the depths of the sea, becoming one with the aquatic environment and gaining heightened senses and mobility.</li>
<li>Maelstrom's Grasp: Create a swirling vortex of water that ensnares and immobilizes enemies, leaving them vulnerable to further attacks.</li>
<li>Waveform Projection: Manipulate the flow of water molecules, shaping them into lethal projectiles that can pierce through armor and defenses.</li>
<li>Leviathan's Call: Summon a massive sea serpent from the depths, whose sheer presence instills fear in your enemies and wreaks havoc upon them.</li>
<li>Tidal Resurgence: Draw upon the rejuvenating powers of the ocean, rapidly restoring your health and revitalizing your stamina.</li>
<li>Tsunami's Wrath: Unleash the might of a colossal tsunami, summoning a devastating tidal wave that crashes upon the battlefield, obliterating everything in its path.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Eldritch_Walker:_Enigmatic_Tideguard">Eldritch Walker: Enigmatic Tideguard</span></h2>
<ol><li>Eldritch Empowerment: Channel eldritch energies into your weapons and armor, infusing them with otherworldly power and enhancing your combat prowess.</li>
<li>Arcane Tide Step: Manipulate the ebb and flow of eldritch energy, allowing for teleportation across the battlefield and surprising your foes.</li>
<li>Abyssal Ward: Create a protective barrier of eldritch energy that shields you from harm, absorbing and redirecting incoming attacks.</li>
<li>Eldritch Blade Dance: Engage in a graceful and deadly dance, wielding your weapon with precision and striking at vulnerable points with eldritch-infused strikes.</li>
<li>Enigmatic Presence: Exude an aura of enigmatic energy that confuses and disorients your enemies, making them more susceptible to your attacks.</li>
<li>Eldritch Shieldbreaker: Unleash a devastating strike that shatters the defenses of your foes, breaking through barriers and nullifying magical protections.</li>
<li>Cursed Invocation: Utter eldritch incantations that curse your enemies, weakening their abilities and leaving them vulnerable to your assaults.</li>
<li>Eldritch Veil: Shroud yourself in an ethereal cloak of eldritch mist, granting temporary invisibility and intangibility, allowing for stealthy maneuvers and surprise attacks.</li>
<li>Voidwalk: Traverse the realms between dimensions, phasing in and out of existence, granting temporary invulnerability and immunity to physical harm.</li>
<li>Eldritch Ascendancy: Embrace your eldritch heritage fully, transforming into a being of pure eldritch energy, granting immense power and unlocking devastating abilities.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Eldritch_Sea_God:_Abyssal_Divinity_of_the_Waves">Eldritch Sea God: Abyssal Divinity of the Waves</span></h2>
<ol><li>Divine Surge: Harness the power of the eldritch sea, summoning surges of primordial energy that engulf your enemies and leave them writhing in agony.</li>
<li>Tentacled Dominion: Manifest writhing tentacles of eldritch power, using them to ensnare and crush your foes with irresistible force.</li>
<li>Eldritch Tideform: Assume a monstrous form, merging with the eldritch sea and gaining increased strength, resilience, and control over eldritch energies.</li>
<li>Abyssal Conduit: Tap into the vast depths of the abyss, channeling its dark energies to unleash devastating spells and curses upon your enemies.</li>
<li>Oceanic Manipulation: Command the very elements of the sea, controlling water, ice, and storms to manipulate the battlefield and drown your enemies in chaos.</li>
<li>Eldritch Enthrallment: Twist the minds of your enemies, ensnaring their thoughts with eldritch whispers and commanding them to do your bidding.</li>
<li>Leviathan's Wrath: Summon the wrath of a colossal eldritch sea creature, whose mere presence causes widespread devastation and terror among your foes.</li>
<li>Cursed Undertow: Generate a malevolent undertow of eldritch energy, pulling enemies towards you and draining their life force with each passing moment.</li>
<li>Eldritch Resurgence: Tap into the regenerative powers of the eldritch sea, rapidly healing your wounds and replenishing your eldritch energy.</li>
<li>Primordial Cataclysm: Unleash a cataclysmic burst of eldritch power, obliterating everything in its path and leaving only desolation and madness in its wake.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Knight_of_Tsunami:_Tempestborne_Warden">Knight of Tsunami: Tempestborne Warden</span></h2>
<ol><li>Tidal Blade Mastery: Master the art of wielding a specially crafted blade infused with the power of the tsunami, striking with deadly precision and force.</li>
<li>Tempest's Resilience: Draw strength from the raging tempest, enhancing your endurance and resistance to physical and magical attacks.</li>
<li>Aqua Step: Move with exceptional speed and agility, deftly evading attacks and positioning yourself strategically on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Wavebreaker's Shield: Summon a shield of solidified water that not only protects you but also repels and deflects incoming projectiles and spells.</li>
<li>Raging Torrent: Unleash a torrential surge of water that engulfs your enemies, causing damage over time and reducing their movement speed.</li>
<li>Tsunami Charge: Charge forward with the force of a tsunami, knocking down enemies in your path and leaving them vulnerable to subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Aqua Blast: Channel your energy into a concentrated blast of water, unleashing a powerful projectile that pierces through enemies and obstacles.</li>
<li>Ebb and Flow: Manipulate the tides of battle, bolstering your allies' strength and healing their wounds while simultaneously weakening and draining your enemies' vitality.</li>
<li>Tempest's Wrath: Call upon the fury of the tempest, conjuring a localized storm that rains down lightning and blasts of water, wreaking havoc on your foes.</li>
<li>Tsunami's Embrace: Embrace the full power of the tsunami, transforming into a tidal wave of destruction that sweeps across the battlefield, crushing all who stand in your way.</li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cell_Storm_Conjurer:_Primal_Elemental_Tempestcaster">Cell Storm Conjurer: Primal Elemental Tempestcaster</span></h2>
<ol><li><ol><li>Elemental Fusion: Merge primal elements of fire, ice, and lightning, creating devastating storms of energy that ravage your enemies.</li>
<li>Tempest Form: Transform into a living embodiment of the tempest, harnessing the raw power of wind and lightning to enhance your speed and agility.</li>
<li>Elemental Burst: Unleash a torrential burst of elemental energy, engulfing your foes in a whirlwind of fire, ice shards, and electric bolts.</li>
<li>Stormweaver's Resilience: Draw upon the chaotic energies of the storm to bolster your defenses, granting temporary invulnerability to elemental damage.</li>
<li>Thunderous Call: Summon the fury of thunder and lightning, creating a cacophony of electrifying bolts that strike down upon your enemies.</li>
<li>Elemental Cyclone: Generate a swirling vortex of elemental energy, pulling enemies towards its center and causing continuous damage as they are battered by the elements.</li>
<li>Primal Infusion: Infuse your weapons with primal energy, imbuing each strike with the power of the storm, causing additional elemental damage.</li>
<li>Lightning Cascade: Unleash a cascade of lightning bolts upon your foes, chaining between multiple targets and leaving a trail of electrified devastation.</li>
<li>Elemental Convergence: Merge all three primal elements into a singular devastating attack, creating a cataclysmic explosion that engulfs the battlefield in elemental chaos.</li>
<li>Tempest Mastery: Attain true mastery over the primal elements, allowing you to manipulate and control storms of unparalleled magnitude, altering the very fabric of reality.</li></ol></li></ol>
<h2><span id="Call_of_the_Enon_Walker:_Enon.27s_Echo.2C_Bearer_of_Waves"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Call_of_the_Enon_Walker:_Enon's_Echo,_Bearer_of_Waves">Call of the Enon Walker: Enon's Echo, Bearer of Waves</span></h2>
<ol><li><ol><li>Enon's Resonance: Harmonize with the ancient echoes of Enon, granting enhanced senses and attunement to the ebb and flow of energy around you.</li>
<li>Wavecaller's Command: Channel the power of Enon's voice, commanding the tides and causing water-based spells to be more potent and devastating.</li>
<li>Echo Step: Step into the ethereal echoes of Enon, allowing for swift and silent movement across the battlefield, evading detection and surprising enemies.</li>
<li>Resounding Surge: Unleash a surge of echoing energy that disorients and stuns your enemies, rendering them vulnerable to follow-up attacks.</li>
<li>Tidebinder's Shield: Form a shield of condensed water infused with Enon's essence, capable of deflecting and absorbing incoming attacks.</li>
<li>Enon's Blessing: Invoke Enon's blessing, restoring health and replenishing magical energy, revitalizing yourself and nearby allies.</li>
<li>Echosphere: Create a sphere of resonating energy, distorting the perception and abilities of your enemies within its radius, weakening their attacks and slowing their movements.</li>
<li>Enon's Embrace: Merge with the echoes of Enon, gaining ethereal form and temporary invulnerability, allowing you to pass through obstacles and evade harm.</li>
<li>Tidecaller's Wrath: Unleash the full fury of Enon's power, summoning a colossal tidal wave that crashes down upon your foes, sweeping them away in its wake.</li>
<li>Enon's Echoes: Channel multiple echoes of Enon simultaneously, creating a symphony of devastating spells and abilities, overwhelming your enemies with a relentless onslaught of water-based attacks.</li></ol></li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Abyssal_One:_Herald_of_the_Deep_Abyss">Abyssal One: Herald of the Deep Abyss</span></h2>
<ol><li><ol><li>Abyssal Invocation: Invoke the ancient powers of the deep abyss, granting you control over darkness and shadow, instilling fear and despair in your enemies.</li>
<li>Abyssal Shroud: Surround yourself in an impenetrable shroud of darkness, rendering you invisible and granting enhanced stealth and surprise capabilities.</li>
<li>Voidstep: Step through the abyss, momentarily entering a shadowy realm, allowing for rapid movement and teleportation across the battlefield.</li>
<li>Abyssal Veil: Manipulate the shadows to create illusions and decoys, confusing and disorienting your enemies, making it difficult for them to discern your true location.</li>
<li>Umbral Drain: Tap into the essence of the abyss, draining the life force of your enemies with each strike, replenishing your own health and magical energy.</li>
<li>Abyssal Terror: Manifest terrifying specters and phantoms from the deep abyss, instilling madness and paralyzing fear in the hearts of those who oppose you.</li>
<li>Dark Embrace: Embrace the darkness of the abyss, temporarily transforming into a shadowy form, gaining increased agility and resistance to physical and magical attacks.</li>
<li>Abyssal Rift: Tear open a rift to the abyss, releasing tendrils of darkness that ensnare and immobilize your enemies, rendering them helpless.</li>
<li>Herald of Desolation: Unleash the true power of the deep abyss, becoming the embodiment of its wrath, enveloping the battlefield in eternal darkness and despair.</li>
<li>Abyssal Annihilation: Tap into the deepest abyssal energies, unleashing a cataclysmic wave of darkness that obliterates everything in its path, leaving nothing but desolation and void in its wake.</li></ol></li></ol>

<ol><li>Divine Foresight: Tap into celestial visions, granting the ability to foresee the immediate future, increasing your chances of evading attacks and predicting enemy movements.</li>

<h2><span id="Knight_of_Beldora:_Beldora.27s_Vowed_Knight"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Knight_of_Beldora:_Beldora's_Vowed_Knight">Knight of Beldora: Beldora's Vowed Knight</span></h2>

<h2><span id="The_Monarch.27s_Host:_Regal_Legionnaire_of_Sovereignty"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Monarch's_Host:_Regal_Legionnaire_of_Sovereignty">The Monarch's Host: Regal Legionnaire of Sovereignty</span></h2>

<li>Royal Decree: Invoke a royal decree that weakens the resolve and combat abilities of your enemies, reducing their damage output and impairing their defenses.</li>
<li>Monarch's Command: Speak with the authority of the monarch, granting enhanced coordination and synergy among your allies, enabling them to execute devastating cooperative maneuvers.</li>
<li>Regal Resurgence: Tap into the power of the monarchy, rejuvenating yourself and nearby allies, restoring health and stamina and purging negative effects.</li>
<li>Majestic Retribution: Unleash the wrath of the monarchy, delivering a devastating attack that strikes fear into the hearts of your enemies and deals increased damage based on the number of allies you are protecting.</li>
<li>Monarch's Ascendancy: Attain the pinnacle of regal power, becoming an embodiment of sovereignty and inspiring unwavering loyalty in your allies, commanding the battlefield with unyielding authority and leading your forces to triumph.</li></ol>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Void_Knight:_Shadowsoul_Vanguard">Void Knight: Shadowsoul Vanguard</span></h2>
<ol><li>Shadowstrike: Unleash swift and precise strikes infused with the power of the void, rending through armor and bypassing defenses to deal substantial damage to your foes.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Shroud yourself in an impenetrable cloak of shadows, becoming invisible to your enemies and gaining enhanced stealth capabilities for a limited duration.</li>
<li>Void Armor: Fortify yourself with an ethereal armor made of pure darkness, granting increased resistance to physical and magical attacks and reducing incoming damage.</li>
<li>Abyssal Charge: Charge forward with relentless determination, leaving behind trails of swirling shadows that disorient and weaken your enemies, while also dealing damage upon impact.</li>
<li>Dark Embrace: Embrace the darkness within, sacrificing a portion of your health to unleash a devastating void explosion, damaging enemies in a wide radius and draining their life force.</li>
<li>Voidstep: Step through the realm of shadows, teleporting instantly to a targeted location and leaving behind a lingering void energy that slows and weakens any enemies caught in its grasp.</li>
<li>Soul Siphon: Harness the power of the void to drain the life force from your enemies, replenishing your own health and increasing your damage output for a limited duration.</li>
<li>Shadowbound Aegis: Summon a spectral shield that absorbs incoming damage and reflects a portion of it back at your assailants, while also providing temporary immunity to control effects.</li>
<li>Void Infusion: Infuse your weapon with the essence of the void, imbuing it with dark energy that enhances its damage and allows it to bypass certain defenses or resistances.</li>
<li>Shadowsoul Ascendancy: Ascend to the apex of shadows, becoming a master of void manipulation and commanding the very essence of darkness, striking fear into the hearts of your enemies and embracing your role as a Shadowsoul Vanguard.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Abyssal_Lord:_Dreadborne_Tyrant">Abyssal Lord: Dreadborne Tyrant</span></h2>
<ol><li>Abyssal Slam: Unleash a mighty slam, causing the ground to tremor beneath you and dealing devastating area-of-effect damage to enemies caught within its radius.</li>
<li>Cursed Aura: Radiate a malevolent aura of darkness and despair, reducing the effectiveness of healing and weakening the resolve of nearby enemies, making them more vulnerable to your attacks.</li>
<li>Dreadful Presence: Exude an aura of terror and intimidation, instilling fear in your enemies and causing them to flee or become paralyzed with dread, granting you a significant advantage in combat.</li>
<li>Demonic Resilience: Tap into the power of the abyss, granting yourself enhanced resilience and damage reduction, allowing you to withstand even the most punishing assaults.</li>
<li>Soul Devourer: Invoke the abyss to devour the souls of fallen enemies, replenishing your health and empowering your attacks with dark energy, increasing their damage and potentially inflicting additional debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Abyssal Chains: Summon ethereal chains from the depths of the abyss, ensnaring and immobilizing your foes, rendering them helpless and vulnerable to your onslaught.</li>
<li>Void Torrent: Unleash a torrent of pure void energy, engulfing your enemies in a swirling maelstrom of darkness, dealing continuous damage over time and draining their life force.</li>
<li>Abyssal Command: Issue a command to the abyssal forces under your control, summoning demonic minions or empowering existing allies, enhancing their combat abilities and turning the tide of battle in your favor.</li>
<li>Netherstep: Step through the veil between realms, momentarily phasing out of existence and reappearing in a new location, allowing for strategic repositioning or swift escapes from danger.</li>
<li>Dreadborne Ascendancy: Ascend to the zenith of dread and become a Dreadborne Tyrant, embodying the fearsome might of the abyss, commanding demonic legions and spreading chaos and destruction wherever you tread.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Covenant.27s_Chosen:_Covenant.27s_Vessel_of_Doom"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Covenant's_Chosen:_Covenant's_Vessel_of_Doom">Covenant's Chosen: Covenant's Vessel of Doom</span></h2>
<ol><li>Divine Retribution: Channel the power of your covenant, inflicting divine punishment upon your enemies and dealing righteous damage that is particularly effective against those who oppose your chosen cause.</li>
<li>Sacred Shield: Summon a shield of divine energy, protecting yourself and nearby allies from harm and providing increased resistance to magical attacks and harmful effects.</li>
<li>Zealous Conviction: Inspire unwavering faith in your cause, bolstering the morale and combat prowess of your allies, increasing their damage output and granting temporary immunity to fear and control effects.</li>
<li>Divine Intervention: Call upon the divine forces of your covenant to intervene on your behalf, granting you a temporary burst of increased power, enhanced healing abilities, and the ability to resurrect fallen allies.</li>
<li>Holy Smite: Unleash a holy smite upon your foes, causing radiant damage and purging them of any dark or corrupting influences, while also granting a restorative effect to nearby allies.</li>
<li>Righteous Fury: Ignite your righteous fury, empowering your attacks with holy energy and causing them to bypass certain defenses or resistances, dealing increased damage to enemies aligned with opposing factions or forces.</li>
<li>Covenant's Blessing: Receive the blessings of your covenant, granting you enhanced regeneration, increased resistance to negative effects, and the ability to dispel curses or ailments from yourself and your allies.</li>
<li>Divine Wrath: Invoke the wrath of your covenant, calling down divine lightning to strike your enemies and inflict massive area-of-effect damage, leaving them stunned and vulnerable.</li>
<li>Sacred Oath: Swear a sacred oath to your covenant, forging an unbreakable bond that grants you additional abilities, boosts your attributes, and imbues your attacks with divine energy, making you a formidable force to be reckoned with.</li>
<li>Covenant's Ascendancy: Ascend to the pinnacle of your covenant's hierarchy, becoming the embodiment of their divine will and leading your faction with unwavering faith and righteous zeal, rallying your allies and vanquishing all who dare to oppose you.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cryptids:_Shadowstalkers_of_the_Unknown">Cryptids: Shadowstalkers of the Unknown</span></h2>
<ol><li>Ephemeral Shift: Dissolve into the shadows, becoming intangible and unseen, allowing you to pass through obstacles and move with unparalleled stealth.</li>
<li>Shadow Claw: Extend your darkened claws with deadly precision, slashing through your enemies and leaving behind lingering shadows that drain their vitality over time.</li>
<li>Chameleon Cloak: Blend seamlessly with your surroundings, adopting the appearance and characteristics of nearby objects or creatures, granting you enhanced camouflage and the ability to deceive your foes.</li>
<li>Phantasmal Howl: Emit a bone-chilling howl that resonates through the realm of shadows, instilling fear and confusion in your enemies, causing them to temporarily lose their sense of direction and coordination.</li>
<li>Umbral Veil: Envelop yourself in an impenetrable shroud of darkness, rendering you immune to targeted abilities and projectiles while also granting increased resistance to magical attacks.</li>
<li>Shadowmeld: Merge with the shadows, becoming completely invisible to both sight and detection spells, allowing you to observe your enemies and plan your next move undetected.</li>
<li>Shadowstep: Traverse vast distances in an instant, teleporting through the shadows to a targeted location, leaving behind a trail of lingering darkness that disorients and slows your enemies.</li>
<li>Shroud of Shadows: Unleash a swirling vortex of shadows around you, obscuring vision and suppressing enemy abilities, making it difficult for them to target or harm you or your allies.</li>
<li>Nightmarish Form: Assume a terrifying and otherworldly visage, causing enemies to be stricken with terror and reducing their combat effectiveness while bolstering your own, granting increased damage and resistance.</li>
<li>Shadows of Chaos: Embrace the true nature of your cryptid lineage, tapping into the boundless power of the shadows, unleashing devastating shadow-based attacks that shatter the minds and bodies of your enemies, leaving them broken and helpless.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Solumn_Warlord:_Grim_Marshal_of_Desolation">Solumn Warlord: Grim Marshal of Desolation</span></h2>
<ol><li>Withering Strike: Deliver a devastating strike imbued with the essence of desolation, draining the life force from your enemies and inflicting a decaying effect that weakens their abilities.</li>
<li>Crippling Aura: Emit an aura of desolation and decay, sapping the strength and vitality of nearby foes, reducing their movement speed and causing their attacks to become sluggish and less effective.</li>
<li>Grim Determination: Tap into your indomitable willpower and unwavering resolve, temporarily gaining increased resistance to control effects and rallying your allies, inspiring them to fight with renewed vigor.</li>
<li>Soul Harvest: Reap the souls of fallen enemies, absorbing their essence to replenish your own health and bolster your physical and magical attributes for a limited time.</li>
<li>Desolate Barrier: Erect a formidable barrier of desolation energy, providing you and your allies with enhanced protection against incoming attacks and reducing the effectiveness of enemy abilities within its radius.</li>
<li>Eclipsing Shadows: Summon a shroud of impenetrable darkness, plunging the battlefield into pitch-black obscurity, disorienting and confusing your enemies while granting you heightened perception and accuracy.</li>
<li>Wail of Despair: Unleash a bone-chilling wail that echoes with the anguish of the desolate, rending the morale of your foes and weakening their resistance to your attacks.</li>
<li>Warlord's Command: Issue a commanding decree, bolstering the morale and combat effectiveness of your allies, granting them increased damage, resistance, and the ability to shrug off debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Desolation's Embrace: Embrace the essence of desolation, transforming into a harbinger of doom, radiating an aura of despair and causing your attacks to leave behind a lingering desolate effect that saps the life force of your enemies.</li>
<li>Ruination's Call: Invoke the ultimate power of desolation, unleashing a cataclysmic wave of energy that obliterates everything in its path, leaving behind a desolate wasteland and ensuring the absolute annihilation of your enemies.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Killer_of_Enon:_Enon.27s_Bane.2C_Harbinger_of_Death"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Killer_of_Enon:_Enon's_Bane,_Harbinger_of_Death">Killer of Enon: Enon's Bane, Harbinger of Death</span></h2>
<ol><li>Enon's Wrath: Unleash the vengeful fury of Enon, inflicting devastating damage upon your enemies and leaving them marked for death, causing them to take increased damage from subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Deathly Precision: Harness the essence of death itself, granting you unparalleled accuracy and precision in combat, allowing your attacks to bypass defenses and strike with lethal efficiency.</li>
<li>Shadowstep Assassin: Vanish into the shadows, reappearing behind your target with uncanny speed and precision, delivering a deadly strike that leaves them gasping for their last breath.</li>
<li>Soul Piercer: Wield a sinister weapon infused with the power of death, piercing through the defenses of your foes and inflicting grievous wounds that drain their life force with each strike.</li>
<li>Death's Embrace: Embrace the inevitability of death, rendering yourself immune to death-related effects and gaining temporary invulnerability, allowing you to continue the relentless pursuit of your enemies without fear.</li>
<li>Grim Harvest: Claim the souls of fallen enemies, absorbing their essence to replenish your own health and bolster your damage output, empowering you with the strength of the fallen.</li>
<li>Harbinger's Presence: Radiate an aura of impending doom, instilling fear and unease in your enemies, causing them to hesitate and falter in their attacks, while granting you increased resilience and resolve.</li>
<li>Death's Gaze: Lock eyes with your foes, invoking a paralyzing fear that immobilizes them in terror, rendering them helpless and susceptible to your deadly onslaught.</li>
<li>Mark of Annihilation: Brand your enemies with a cursed mark, amplifying the damage they receive and making them vulnerable to your attacks, ensuring their swift demise.</li>
<li>Requiem's Echo: Channel the echoes of the fallen, unleashing a devastating wave of necrotic energy that consumes all life in its path, leaving behind a desolate aftermath and heralding the arrival of death itself.</li></ol>
<ol><li>Abyssal Scythe: Wield a wicked scythe infused with the essence of the abyss, reaping the souls of your enemies and harvesting their life force with each strike.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Shroud yourself in a cloak of swirling shadows, granting you enhanced stealth and reducing your visibility to your foes, allowing you to move undetected and strike from the shadows.</li>
<li>Soul Siphon: Tap into the dark energies of the abyss, draining the life force of nearby enemies to replenish your own health and bolster your strength, becoming even more formidable as the souls of your victims empower you.</li>
<li>Voidstep: Traverse through the void, seamlessly moving between dimensions and appearing at your desired location, leaving behind a trail of dark energy that disorients and weakens those who dare to follow.</li>
<li>Abyssal Conduit: Become a conduit of abyssal power, channeling the raw energy of the abyss to unleash devastating spells and abilities that rend the very fabric of reality, decimating your enemies in the process.</li>
<li>Ephemeral Existence: Manipulate the boundaries between life and death, rendering yourself ethereal and untouchable for a brief period, evading attacks and gaining immunity to harmful effects.</li>
<li>Grim Retribution: Invoke the wrath of the abyss, causing enemies who harm you to be stricken with a curse that slowly drains their life force, punishing them for their audacity.</li>
<li>Necrotic Affliction: Inflict a vile affliction upon your enemies, corrupting their life force and causing them to wither away over time, weakening their defenses and leaving them vulnerable to your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Abyssal Embrace: Embrace the darkness of the abyss, granting yourself increased resistance to physical and magical attacks, while empowering your own strikes with a touch of the abyssal void.</li>
<li>Oblivion's Call: Unleash the ultimate manifestation of the abyss, summoning a cataclysmic vortex that tears through reality itself, engulfing everything in its path and consigning your enemies to eternal oblivion.</li></ol>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Mind_Flayer:_Psykron_Enigma">Mind Flayer: Psykron Enigma</span></h2>
<ol><li>Psychic Siphon: Draw upon the psychic energies of your enemies, draining their thoughts and memories to replenish your own mental prowess.</li>
<li>Mind Blast: Unleash a devastating psychic shockwave, causing intense pain and disorientation to your foes, leaving them vulnerable to your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Neural Domination: Assert your dominance over the minds of others, bending their will to your own and compelling them to carry out your bidding.</li>
<li>Telepathic Illusions: Manipulate the perceptions of your enemies, creating vivid illusions that deceive their senses and lead them astray.</li>
<li>Psychic Shield: Erect a powerful mental barrier around yourself, warding off psychic attacks and granting you increased resistance against mental manipulation.</li>
<li>Mental Scrying: Peer into the thoughts and memories of individuals, uncovering hidden information and unraveling their deepest secrets.</li>
<li>Psionic Levitation: Harness the power of your mind to levitate and maneuver effortlessly through the air, granting you enhanced mobility and the ability to evade ground-based attacks.</li>
<li>Psychic Feedback: Reflect the psychic attacks of your enemies back at them, turning their own mental assaults into a weapon against themselves.</li>
<li>Psychic Invisibility: Camouflage your presence by manipulating the minds of those around you, rendering yourself invisible to both sight and telepathic detection.</li>
<li>Thought Eater: Consume the thoughts and mental energy of your enemies, leaving them mentally drained and disoriented while bolstering your own psychic abilities.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Deepbounded:_Abyssal_Nexus_Guardian">Deepbounded: Abyssal Nexus Guardian</span></h2>
<ol><li>Abyssal Resilience: Tap into the power of the abyss to enhance your physical and magical defenses, rendering yourself highly resistant to damage from both mundane and supernatural sources.</li>
<li>Darkwater Veil: Envelop yourself in a swirling shroud of darkwater, rendering you partially incorporeal and allowing you to pass through solid objects.</li>
<li>Abyssal Wave: Summon a massive wave of darkwater to crash upon your enemies, drenching them in the abyssal essence and causing disorientation and vulnerability.</li>
<li>Tentacle Grasp: Extend your darkwater-infused tentacles to ensnare and restrain your foes, rendering them immobile and susceptible to your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Abyssal Command: Command the creatures of the abyss to heed your call, summoning darkwater abominations and twisted denizens to fight alongside you in battle.</li>
<li>Abyssal Healing: Draw upon the rejuvenating properties of the abyssal waters to heal your wounds and mend your injuries, rapidly regenerating lost vitality.</li>
<li>Cursed Eruption: Unleash a torrent of cursed energy from the depths of the abyss, causing devastating explosions and widespread destruction to engulf your enemies.</li>
<li>Abyssal Shift: Phase in and out of the abyssal realm, allowing you to teleport short distances and surprise your foes with sudden and unpredictable movements.</li>
<li>Darkwater Infusion: Infuse your weapons and attacks with the essence of the abyss, causing them to deal additional dark and corrosive damage to your enemies.</li>
<li>Abyssal Convergence: Unleash the full might of the abyss, causing a cataclysmic convergence of darkwater energy that devastates everything in its path, leaving behind a trail of desolation and chaos.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Eldritch_Vistage:_Veilstorm_Conjurer">Eldritch Vistage: Veilstorm Conjurer</span></h2>
<ol><li>Veilstorm Manipulation: Harness the raw power of the veilstorm, shaping its chaotic energies to serve your will, manipulating reality and distorting the fabric of space and time.</li>
<li>Elemental Tempest: Summon and control the elemental forces within the veilstorm, conjuring raging storms of fire, lightning, ice, and wind to devastate your enemies.</li>
<li>Arcane Disruption: Disrupt the flow of magic and disrupt the spells and abilities of your adversaries, rendering them powerless and vulnerable to your attacks.</li>
<li>Veilstep: Step through the veil between dimensions, briefly phasing out of existence to avoid attacks or reposition yourself strategically on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Veilstorm Nova: Unleash a cataclysmic burst of veilstorm energy, creating a powerful explosion that engulfs your foes, inflicting immense damage and chaos.</li>
<li>Dimensional Rift: Tear open a rift in reality, creating a portal that connects distant locations and allows you and your allies to swiftly traverse the battlefield.</li>
<li>Chaosweaver's Grasp: Manipulate the veilstorm's chaotic threads, ensnaring your enemies in a web of entropic energy, restricting their movements and weakening their attacks.</li>
<li>Veilward: Fortify your defenses with a shimmering veil of energy, deflecting incoming attacks and rendering yourself resistant to damage from both physical and magical sources.</li>
<li>Tempestuous Affliction: Infuse your spells and attacks with veilstorm energy, imbuing them with unpredictable and volatile effects that can cripple and debilitate your enemies.</li>
<li>Veilstorm Convergence: Reach the pinnacle of your veilstorm mastery, merging your essence with the chaotic energies, transforming into an avatar of raw power, capable of unleashing devastating forces that can reshape reality itself.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Call_of_The_Deep:_Abysswalker_Herald">Call of The Deep: Abysswalker Herald</span></h2>
<ol><li>Deep Resonance: Tap into the ancient echoes of the deep, channeling its power to enhance your physical attributes and grant you heightened strength, speed, and endurance.</li>
<li>Aquatic Adaptation: Adapt to the depths of the sea, allowing you to breathe underwater, swim with incredible agility, and withstand extreme water pressures.</li>
<li>Siren's Call: Emit a haunting and irresistible melody that captivates and enthralls your enemies, leaving them vulnerable to manipulation or open to attack.</li>
<li>Tidal Surge: Summon a massive tidal wave to crash upon your foes, drowning them in the relentless power of the ocean and causing widespread devastation.</li>
<li>Deepwater Veil: Envelop yourself in a shimmering veil of deepwater, rendering you nigh invisible and granting you enhanced stealth and evasion abilities.</li>
<li>Aqua Manipulation: Command and manipulate water to bend it to your will, creating barriers, launching powerful water projectiles, or even turning water into a deadly weapon.</li>
<li>Abyssal Enchantment: Infuse your weapons and attacks with the essence of the abyss, causing them to deal additional water and corrosive damage to your enemies.</li>
<li>Marine Bond: Forge a deep bond with the creatures of the sea, gaining their loyalty and assistance in battle, whether it be through summoning aquatic allies or receiving aid from powerful sea creatures.</li>
<li>Tidal Empowerment: Harness the power of the tides, allowing you to tap into their ebb and flow, enhancing your abilities and granting you heightened control over water-based magic.</li>
<li>Abysswalker's Legacy: Embrace your role as the herald of the deep, fully embracing the powers bestowed upon you by the abyss, transforming into a fearsome avatar of aquatic might, commanding the oceans to rise and fall at your command.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Deep:_Abyssal_Abomination">The Deep: Abyssal Abomination</span></h2>
<ol><li>Abyssal Maw: Unleash a horrific and all-consuming abyssal maw, capable of devouring and absorbing the life force and essence of your enemies, empowering you and rendering them helpless.</li>
<li>Ominous Presence: Radiate an aura of primordial darkness and dread, striking fear into the hearts of your foes and weakening their resolve.</li>
<li>Tentacle Barrage: Extend an array of writhing abyssal tentacles from your form, lashing out at your enemies with incredible speed and precision, shredding them with eldritch force.</li>
<li>Voidborne Armor: Shroud yourself in impenetrable darkness, manifesting an otherworldly armor that shields you from harm and makes you impervious to conventional attacks.</li>
<li>Shadowstep: Merge with the shadows, allowing you to traverse through dimensions and reappear at a different location, catching your enemies off guard and granting you unparalleled mobility.</li>
<li>Abyssal Corruption: Infect the minds of your foes with the insidious taint of the abyss, driving them to madness and causing them to turn against their allies.</li>
<li>Eldritch Form: Assume a monstrous and nightmarish form, distorting your physicality and granting you immense strength, resilience, and destructive power.</li>
<li>Void Rift: Tear open portals to the void, unleashing torrents of pure darkness and chaos upon your enemies, tearing them apart and causing widespread devastation.</li>
<li>Devouring Shadows: Merge with the shadows themselves, becoming one with the darkness and gaining the ability to teleport instantly between shadows, striking with deadly precision.</li>
<li>Abyssal Cataclysm: Unleash the full might of the abyss, causing a cataclysmic eruption of darkness and destruction, swallowing everything in its path and leaving nothing but desolation and despair in its wake.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Barnon_Hunter:_Dreadstalk_Warden">Barnon Hunter: Dreadstalk Warden</span></h2>
<ol><li>Shadowstep: Merge with the shadows, allowing you to move swiftly and silently across the battlefield, appearing behind your enemies with deadly precision.</li>
<li>Fear's Embrace: Inflict paralyzing fear upon your adversaries, causing them to tremble and hesitate, rendering them vulnerable to your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Envelop yourself in a shroud of darkness, rendering you nearly invisible and granting you enhanced stealth capabilities, making it difficult for your enemies to detect your presence.</li>
<li>Precision Strikes: Strike with calculated precision, exploiting the weak points of your opponents and inflicting devastating critical hits that bypass their defenses.</li>
<li>Poisoned Blades: Coat your weapons with deadly toxins, ensuring that even the slightest graze causes excruciating pain and debilitating effects, weakening your foes over time.</li>
<li>Crippling Shadows: Manipulate the shadows to constrict and bind your enemies, immobilizing them and draining their strength, making them easy prey for your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Shadowmeld: Blend seamlessly with the darkness, becoming one with the shadows and allowing you to evade detection and escape dangerous situations.</li>
<li>Dark Ambush: Strike from the shadows with lightning speed, launching a surprise attack that leaves your enemies disoriented and vulnerable to follow-up assaults.</li>
<li>Shadow's Whisper: Tap into the dark secrets whispered by the shadows, gaining valuable information about your enemies' weaknesses and vulnerabilities.</li>
<li>Shadow Dance: Enter a trance-like state, moving with unparalleled grace and agility, allowing you to dodge incoming attacks with uncanny ease while launching counterattacks with deadly precision.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Voided_Deep_One:_Voidspawn_Dreadbringer">Voided Deep One: Voidspawn Dreadbringer</span></h2>
<ol><li>Void Bolt: Hurl bolts of pure void energy at your enemies, tearing through their defenses and leaving behind a trail of desolation in their wake.</li>
<li>Abyssal Empowerment: Draw upon the power of the void to enhance your physical attributes and augment your attacks with dark and corrosive energy, making them even more devastating.</li>
<li>Voidwalk: Step into the void, traversing through the ethereal realm and reappearing at a different location, allowing you to quickly reposition yourself or catch your enemies off guard.</li>
<li>Void Shield: Manifest an impenetrable shield of void energy, deflecting incoming attacks and rendering yourself invulnerable to harm for a short period.</li>
<li>Annihilation Beam: Unleash a concentrated beam of pure void energy, obliterating everything in its path and causing catastrophic damage to even the sturdiest of opponents.</li>
<li>Voidmeld: Blend your essence with the void, becoming one with the shadows and rendering yourself invisible to both sight and magical detection.</li>
<li>Abyssal Consumption: Absorb the life force and energy of defeated foes, replenishing your own strength and rejuvenating your vitality in the process.</li>
<li>Void Rift: Tear open rifts to the void, summoning forth eldritch entities and horrors to fight alongside you, overwhelming your enemies with sheer numbers and dark power.</li>
<li>Warp Strike: Teleport instantaneously through the void, appearing next to your enemies in a blink of an eye and striking them with devastating force before vanishing again.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Parasitic_Mage:_Symbiotic_Arcanist">Parasitic Mage: Symbiotic Arcanist</span></h2>
<ol><li>Soul Drain: Tap into the life force of your enemies, siphoning their vitality and converting it into raw arcane energy to empower your spells and abilities.</li>
<li>Symbiotic Bond: Form a symbiotic connection with an otherworldly entity, merging your consciousness and sharing your arcane powers, granting you enhanced spellcasting abilities and heightened resilience.</li>
<li>Necrotic Infusion: Infuse your spells and attacks with necrotic energy, causing them to drain the life force of your enemies and leaving them weakened and vulnerable.</li>
<li>Arcane Parasite: Release a parasitic entity into the battlefield, which attaches to enemies and slowly drains their magical essence, transferring it to you and bolstering your own arcane powers.</li>
<li>Mind Manipulation: Manipulate the thoughts and perceptions of your foes, implanting suggestions and illusions to control their actions and turn them against each other.</li>
<li>Arcane Venom: Infuse your spells with toxic arcane energy, causing them to inflict lingering damage over time and weaken the defenses of your enemies.</li>
<li>Life Tap: Sacrifice a portion of your own vitality to fuel your spells, allowing you to cast powerful magic beyond your normal limits.</li>
<li>Arcane Resonance: Create a field of arcane energy that resonates with your spells, amplifying their effects and causing them to cascade and ricochet between enemies.</li>
<li>Arcane Absorption: Absorb incoming magical attacks and convert them into additional power for your own spells, rendering you immune to magical harm while simultaneously empowering yourself.</li>
<li>Symbiotic Ascendance: Achieve a state of symbiotic transcendence, merging your physical form with the essence of the arcane, granting you temporary godlike power and the ability to reshape reality itself.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Leechlin:_Soulreaver_Leechkin">Leechlin: Soulreaver Leechkin</span></h2>
<ol><li>Essence Drain: Leech the life force of your enemies with every strike, replenishing your own vitality and growing stronger with each feeding.</li>
<li>Soul Tether: Bind your life force to that of your target, causing them to share in your pain and suffering, leaving them weakened and drained while bolstering your own resilience.</li>
<li>Blood Leech: Drain the blood of your foes, using it to regenerate your own wounds and enhance your physical abilities, increasing your strength, speed, and endurance.</li>
<li>Vampiric Strike: Infuse your weapon with vampiric energy, allowing your attacks to steal the life essence of your enemies, healing yourself and weakening them in the process.</li>
<li>Shadow Cloak: Shroud yourself in an ethereal cloak of darkness, rendering you partially incorporeal and making it difficult for your enemies to land solid strikes against you.</li>
<li>Blood Pact: Form a pact with dark entities, binding them to your service and gaining their assistance in battle, whether it be through empowering your attacks or providing you with additional abilities.</li>
<li>Soul Shackle: Constrict the souls of your enemies, trapping them within ethereal chains and rendering them immobile, allowing you to freely attack or escape from their grasp.</li>
<li>Life Tap: Channel your own life force into your spells and abilities, sacrificing a portion of your vitality to enhance their potency and ensure their success.</li>
<li>Dark Transfusion: Transfer your own life force to your allies, healing their wounds and granting them temporary increased strength and resilience.</li>
<li>Soulreaver's Ascendance: Embrace your true nature as a Soulreaver, transcending the boundaries of mortality and becoming an ethereal entity of pure hunger and destruction, capable of annihilating your enemies and absorbing their very essence to fuel your own power.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Puppeteer:_Strings_of_Dominion">Puppeteer: Strings of Dominion</span></h2>
<ol><li>Marionette Control: Manipulate the bodies of your enemies like puppets on strings, forcing them to perform your bidding and turning them against their allies.</li>
<li>String Snare: Ensnare your enemies with ethereal strings, immobilizing them and leaving them vulnerable to your subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Puppet Master's Will: Establish a mental link with your controlled puppets, allowing you to perceive through their senses and control them with greater precision and efficiency.</li>
<li>String Slash: Unleash a flurry of razor-sharp strings, slashing through the air and slicing through your enemies, causing deep lacerations and leaving them bleeding.</li>
<li>Marionette Shield: Form a protective shield of animated strings around yourself, deflecting incoming attacks and rendering you immune to harm while you manipulate your puppets.</li>
<li>Puppeteer's Command: Issue precise commands to your puppets, instructing them to perform complex actions, such as disarming enemies, retrieving objects, or setting up traps.</li>
<li>String Constriction: Tighten the strings around your enemies, constricting their movements and exerting pressure on their bodies, causing both physical and psychological discomfort.</li>
<li>Puppeteer's Reflection: Create illusionary duplicates of yourself using ethereal strings, confusing your enemies and making it difficult for them to determine your true location.</li>
<li>Marionette Swarm: Summon a horde of smaller puppets, overwhelming your enemies with their sheer numbers and coordinating their attacks for maximum efficiency.</li>
<li>Master of Marionettes: Attain mastery over your puppeteering abilities, allowing you to control multiple puppets simultaneously, each with their own unique skills and attributes, turning the battlefield into a chaotic stage of your design.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Robotic_Parasite:_Mechanothral_Infiltrator">Robotic Parasite: Mechanothral Infiltrator</span></h2>
<ol><li>Techno-Drain: Inflict cybernetic parasites upon your enemies, draining the energy from their technological implants and disabling their equipment.</li>
<li>Neural Hijack: Override the neural interfaces of robotic beings, seizing control of their motor functions and turning them into unwitting allies or causing them to self-destruct.</li>
<li>Nanomachine Infusion: Integrate nanomachines into your own body, enhancing your physical capabilities, repairing injuries, and granting you resistance to various forms of damage.</li>
<li>Tech Disruption: Emit disruptive electromagnetic pulses, temporarily disabling nearby electronic devices and disrupting the functionality of technological weapons and defenses.</li>
<li>Stealth Circuitry: Activate your stealth mode, allowing you to blend seamlessly into your surroundings and become nearly invisible to both visual and technological detection.</li>
<li>Techno-Sabotage: Manipulate the internal systems of mechanical devices, causing them to malfunction, overload, or even explode, creating chaos on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Cybernetic Augmentation: Enhance your own body with cybernetic implants, granting you increased strength, speed, and sensory perception, as well as the ability to interface with and control various forms of technology.</li>
<li>Holographic Decoy: Project a lifelike holographic duplicate of yourself, diverting enemy attention and allowing you to maneuver strategically or launch surprise attacks.</li>
<li>System Override: Override the control systems of enemy robots and drones, turning them against their own allies or using them as distractions or shields.</li>
<li>Mechanized Dominance: Attain complete mastery over technology, allowing you to merge with machinery, manipulate complex systems with ease, and even transform into a powerful mechanized form, becoming an unstoppable force of technological destruction.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Enon.27s_Creation:_Enonforged_Champion"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Enon's_Creation:_Enonforged_Champion">Enon's Creation: Enonforged Champion</span></h2>
<ol><li>Enon's Resilience: Harness the resilience and durability of your construct form, rendering yourself highly resistant to physical and magical attacks.</li>
<li>Enon's Fury: Unleash a torrent of devastating attacks, fueled by the raw power of Enon, striking fear into the hearts of your enemies and leaving destruction in your wake.</li>
<li>Constructive Adaptation: Modify your construct body on the fly, altering your physical form to gain temporary enhancements such as increased strength, additional limbs, or improved sensory capabilities.</li>
<li>Enon's Command: Establish a direct mental link with Enon, allowing you to communicate with the ancient being and receive guidance, wisdom, or even direct commands in times of need.</li>
<li>Elemental Affinity: Infuse your attacks with elemental energy, channeling the power of fire, ice, lightning, or other elemental forces to deal devastating damage and inflict debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Enon's Reconstruction: Repair and regenerate your construct body, mending damaged parts and restoring lost functionality, ensuring your continued presence on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Construct Arsenal: Summon an arsenal of specialized weapons and tools from within your construct body, ranging from powerful energy cannons to versatile utility devices, allowing you to adapt to various combat situations.</li>
<li>Enon's Embrace: Temporarily merge with Enon, becoming one with the ancient being and gaining access to its vast knowledge and power, exponentially increasing your combat capabilities.</li>
<li>Unyielding Core: Strengthen your construct core, fortifying your resilience against attacks and rendering you immune to debilitating effects such as paralysis or mind control.</li>
<li>Enonforged Ascendance: Achieve the ultimate stage of your transformation, fully merging with Enon and transcending your mortal limitations, becoming a towering and unstoppable force of destruction and annihilation.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Host_of_Velial:_Velial.27s_Possessed_Adept"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Host_of_Velial:_Velial's_Possessed_Adept">Host of Velial: Velial's Possessed Adept</span></h2>
<ol><li>Infernal Pact: Forge a symbiotic bond with Velial, granting you access to dark powers and enhancing your own magical abilities.</li>
<li>Shadow Veil: Shroud yourself in a cloak of shadows, granting you enhanced stealth and making you difficult to detect or target by enemies.</li>
<li>Soul Corruption: Infuse your attacks with Velial's corruptive energy, sapping the life force from your foes and leaving them weakened and vulnerable.</li>
<li>Forbidden Knowledge: Tap into Velial's ancient knowledge, gaining insights into forbidden spells and rituals that grant you powerful and forbidden abilities.</li>
<li>Demonic Possession: Temporarily allow Velial to possess your body, granting you increased strength, agility, and dark magic, but also risking losing control to the entity within.</li>
<li>Infernal Resilience: Channel Velial's infernal power to bolster your physical and magical defenses, making you highly resistant to both physical and magical attacks.</li>
<li>Abyssal Surge: Unleash a surge of dark energy, creating a shockwave that damages and disrupts the essence of your enemies, causing chaos and disarray on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Pact Binding: Bind lesser demons or otherworldly entities to your will, forcing them to serve you and fight alongside you as loyal minions.</li>
<li>Velial's Mark: Brand your enemies with Velial's dark mark, making them vulnerable to your subsequent attacks and empowering your spells and curses against them.</li>
<li>Ascension of Velial: Embrace the full power of Velial, allowing the entity to possess you completely and transforming you into an avatar of darkness, radiating terror and devastation to all who oppose you.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Knight_Velial:_Velial.27s_Vengeance_Incarnate"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Knight_Velial:_Velial's_Vengeance_Incarnate">Knight Velial: Velial's Vengeance Incarnate</span></h2>

<li>Darksteel Armor: Summon an impenetrable armor infused with Velial's dark energy, granting you enhanced protection and resilience in battle.</li>
<li>Soulblade Mastery: Wield a wicked and ethereal blade imbued with Velial's power, slashing through your foes with supernatural speed and precision.</li>
<li>Cursed Strikes: Inflict curses upon your enemies with each strike, weakening their defenses, draining their strength, or inducing debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Shadowstep: Blink through the shadows, appearing behind your enemies in an instant and launching surprise attacks from unexpected angles.</li>
<li>Velial's Blessing: Receive Velial's blessing, enhancing your physical abilities, boosting your combat prowess, and heightening your senses for a limited time.</li>
<li>Avatar of Vengeance: Tap into Velial's vengeful essence, transforming into a menacing and ethereal form that radiates darkness and strikes fear into the hearts of your foes.</li>
<li>Oath of Retribution: Swear an oath to exact vengeance upon those who have wronged you or your allies, gaining a temporary power boost and increased resistance against their attacks.</li>
<li>Unyielding Will: Draw upon Velial's indomitable willpower, shrugging off mental and emotional attacks, and becoming immune to fear and intimidation.</li>
<li>Velial's Judgment: Invoke Velial's judgment upon your enemies, calling down a devastating wave of dark energy that engulfs the battlefield, obliterating all who stand against you.</li></ol>
