<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Berserker:_Rampaging_Cataclysm">Berserker: Rampaging Cataclysm</span></h2>
<ol><li>Frenzied Onslaught: Unleash a relentless and chaotic flurry of attacks, striking with incredible speed and ferocity, overwhelming your enemies.</li>
<li>Savage Roar: Emit a primal roar that instills fear and panic in your foes, weakening their resolve and disrupting their actions.</li>
<li>Bloodlust Surge: Tap into your inner rage, granting temporary boosts to your strength, speed, and endurance, making you an unstoppable force on the battlefield.</li>
<li>Raging Tempest: Channel the raw power of the storm, summoning a whirlwind of destructive energy that tears through your enemies and leaves devastation in its wake.</li>
<li>Unstoppable Momentum: Build up an unstoppable momentum with each successful attack, allowing you to shrug off incoming damage and push through enemy defenses.</li>
<li>Berserker's Resilience: Embrace pain and injuries as a source of strength, gaining increased resistance to damage and shrugging off debilitating effects.</li>
<li>Cataclysmic Cleave: Unleash a devastating sweeping strike that cleaves through multiple enemies, causing widespread damage and leaving them vulnerable to follow-up attacks.</li>
<li>Battle Trance: Enter a trance-like state of heightened focus and awareness, enhancing your combat instincts and granting superior accuracy and precision in your strikes.</li>
<li>Wrathful Reprisal: Retaliate with unbridled fury against those who dare to harm you, dealing devastating counterattacks that punish your enemies for their aggression.</li>
<li>Annihilation Rampage: Enter a state of pure carnage and destruction, temporarily becoming an unstoppable force of devastation, wreaking havoc on the battlefield and obliterating all who stand in your way.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Planet_Destroyer:_Celestial_Annihilator">Planet Destroyer: Celestial Annihilator</span></h2>
<ol><li>Cosmic Cataclysm: Unleash a devastating surge of celestial energy, causing cataclysmic explosions that engulf the battlefield and decimate everything in its radius.</li>
<li>Stellar Convergence: Manipulate the gravitational forces of nearby celestial bodies, creating a powerful gravitational vortex that draws enemies in and crushes them under immense pressure.</li>
<li>Celestial Shield: Summon a protective barrier of cosmic energy, shielding you from harm and reflecting incoming attacks back at your enemies.</li>
<li>Nova Fury: Tap into the raw power of a dying star, igniting your body with intense stellar energy and radiating destructive waves that incinerate everything in your path.</li>
<li>Astral Projection: Project your consciousness into the astral plane, allowing you to traverse great distances instantaneously and strike at your enemies from unexpected angles.</li>
<li>Cosmic Singularity: Create a miniature black hole that sucks enemies towards its center, trapping them in an inescapable void of crushing gravity and annihilating them.</li>
<li>Celestial Ascendance: Ascend to a higher plane of existence, temporarily becoming one with the cosmos and gaining immense power and enlightenment.</li>
<li>Solar Flare Barrage: Summon a barrage of searing solar flares from distant stars, raining down upon your enemies and causing massive explosions and burns.</li>
<li>Galactic Collapse: Manipulate the fabric of space-time itself, causing a localized collapse that tears through reality, rending your enemies asunder and leaving them in a state of cosmic disarray.</li>
<li>Celestial Obliteration: Channel the energy of a supernova, releasing an explosive surge of cosmic power that obliterates everything within its path, leaving behind only cosmic dust and remnants of destruction.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Nuke_Assimilator:_Atomic_Devourer">Nuke Assimilator: Atomic Devourer</span></h2>
<ol><li>Nuclear Fusion: Harness the power of atomic fusion, generating a devastating explosion that engulfs your enemies in a fiery inferno.</li>
<li>Radioactive Surge: Emit a wave of radiation that contaminates the battlefield, causing damage over time to anyone caught within its toxic embrace.</li>
<li>Atomic Absorption: Absorb atomic energy from nearby nuclear sources, replenishing your own power and rejuvenating your vitality.</li>
<li>Contagion Catalyst: Infuse your attacks with radioactive particles, infecting your foes with a debilitating radiation sickness that weakens their defenses.</li>
<li>Chain Reaction: Trigger a chain reaction among your enemies, causing their bodies to implode in a cataclysmic burst of atomic energy.</li>
<li>Meltdown Nova: Unleash a concentrated burst of nuclear energy, creating a localized nuclear meltdown that melts away defenses and disintegrates your foes.</li>
<li>Atomizer Beam: Emit a focused beam of atomic particles, disintegrating anything it touches and leaving behind nothing but atomic residue.</li>
<li>Nuclear Fallout: Summon a radioactive cloud that engulfs the battlefield, poisoning your enemies and causing gradual deterioration of their health and abilities.</li>
<li>Nuclear Regeneration: Tap into the regenerative properties of atomic energy, rapidly healing your wounds and recovering from injuries with remarkable speed.</li>
<li>Atomic Devourer: Enter a state of atomic frenzy, consuming atomic energy from your surroundings and transforming into an unstoppable force of destruction, obliterating everything in your path.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Particalor_Splitor:_Quantum_Blade_Dancer">Particalor Splitor: Quantum Blade Dancer</span></h2>
<ol><li>Quantum Step: Manipulate quantum particles to move at incredible speeds, allowing you to dash and evade with unparalleled agility and grace.</li>
<li>Photon Slash: Unleash a flurry of rapid slashes infused with quantum energy, leaving behind afterimages that strike your enemies multiple times.</li>
<li>Dimensional Displacement: Shift between dimensions, momentarily becoming intangible and untouchable, bypassing enemy defenses and attacks.</li>
<li>Rift Blade: Create a temporal rift that engulfs your weapon, enhancing its cutting power and allowing you to slice through space itself.</li>
<li>Quantum Echo: Leave behind quantum duplicates of yourself that confuse and disorient your enemies, making it difficult for them to track your true location.</li>
<li>Quantum Barrier: Generate a barrier of unstable quantum energy, absorbing incoming attacks and reflecting them back at your assailants.</li>
<li>Chrono Step: Manipulate time to your advantage, briefly accelerating your own movements while slowing down your enemies, granting you superior speed and reaction time.</li>
<li>Singularity Slash: Infuse your blade with gravitational energy, causing each strike to create miniature singularities that implode upon impact, devastating your foes.</li>
<li>Quantum Parry: Predict your enemies' movements with uncanny precision, allowing you to effortlessly parry and counter their attacks with swift and calculated strikes.</li>
<li>Quantum Blade Dance: Enter a state of perfect synchronization with the quantum realm, becoming an ethereal blur of blades and quantum energy, unleashing a mesmerizing dance of death that leaves your enemies bewildered and defeated.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Beldora.27s_Chosen:_Beldora.27s_Ascendant"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Beldora's_Chosen:_Beldora's_Ascendant">Beldora's Chosen: Beldora's Ascendant</span></h2>

<li>Sacred Conviction: Strengthen your resolve and bolster your allies' faith, increasing their morale, resistance to negative influences, and granting them a temporary boost in combat prowess.</li>
<li>Celestial Beacon: Summon a radiant beacon of light that guides and empowers your allies, enhancing their abilities and granting them divine protection.</li>
<li>Divine Judgment: Channel Beldora's divine judgment upon your enemies, unleashing a devastating wave of holy energy that purges evil and smites the wicked.</li>
<li>Seraphic Wings: Manifest ethereal wings of light, granting you the ability to soar through the air, evading attacks and striking from above with righteous fury.</li>
<li>Beldora's Ascendance: Ascend to the pinnacle of divine power, embodying Beldora's divine essence and becoming a living embodiment of righteousness and holy might.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Star_Buster:_Celestial_Scion_of_Nova">Star Buster: Celestial Scion of Nova</span></h2>
<ol><li>Celestial Blast: Harness the power of the stars, launching concentrated bursts of stellar energy that explode upon impact, obliterating your foes.</li>

<li>Celestial Empowerment: Draw upon the celestial energies of distant stars, enhancing your strength, speed, and resilience, allowing you to unleash your true potential.</li>
<li>Cosmic Rift: Tear open a rift in the fabric of space, summoning cosmic energy that warps reality, distorting and weakening your enemies.</li>
<li>Celestial Nova: Unleash a cataclysmic nova of celestial energy, creating a blinding burst of light that engulfs the battlefield, disorienting and burning your foes.</li>
<li>Astral Projection: Project your consciousness into the astral plane, traversing the cosmos at will, gaining insights and unlocking hidden knowledge.</li>
<li>Stellar Manipulation: Manipulate the gravitational forces of stars, causing celestial bodies to collide and unleash devastating cosmic cataclysms upon your enemies.</li>
<li>Celestial Veil: Surround yourself with a shimmering celestial barrier, deflecting incoming attacks and providing protection against magical and physical threats.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Reality_Crusher:_Existential_Obliterator">Reality Crusher: Existential Obliterator</span></h2>

<li>Entropic Wave: Unleash a wave of entropic energy that decays and erodes everything it touches, reducing matter and energy to their most basic and unstable forms.</li>
<li>Reality Warp: Bend and warp the fabric of reality, altering the environment to your advantage and manipulating space and time to confound and disorient your foes.</li>
<li>Cosmic Annihilation: Channel the immense power of cosmic forces to unleash a cataclysmic blast that annihilates everything in its path, obliterating matter and erasing existence itself.</li>
<li>Existential Shift: Shift your own existence, becoming ethereal and intangible, allowing you to phase through solid objects and evade attacks with ease.</li>
<li>Chaotic Eruption: Trigger a chaotic eruption of reality-altering energy, causing unpredictable and devastating effects to manifest randomly within the battlefield.</li>
<li>Existential Obliteration: Embrace the true nature of your power as an existential obliterator, unleashing an unstoppable onslaught that tears through the very fabric of reality, erasing all traces of existence in its wake.</li></ol>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Energy_Transmuter:_Celestial_Energist">Energy Transmuter: Celestial Energist</span></h2>
<ol><li>Energy Conversion: Harness the raw essence of energy to convert and manipulate various forms of energy, allowing you to absorb, redirect, and enhance its properties.</li>
<li>Elemental Fusion: Merge different elemental energies together, creating potent combinations that unleash devastating attacks or grant powerful elemental enhancements to yourself and allies.</li>
<li>Energy Infusion: Infuse your weapons or projectiles with concentrated energy, empowering them with destructive force, elemental properties, or the ability to bypass defenses.</li>

<li>Celestial Channeling: Establish a direct connection to celestial realms, drawing upon celestial energy to amplify your own power, granting heightened abilities and transcendent energy manipulation.</li>
<li>Energy Transfusion: Transfer your own energy to replenish and heal the energy reserves of yourself and allies, revitalizing their powers and restoring their strength.</li>
<li>Quantum Resonance: Attune yourself to the underlying quantum vibrations of reality, allowing you to phase through energy barriers, manipulate quantum particles, and disrupt energy-based systems.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Eldritch_Energy_Knight:_Arcane_Luminary_of_Eldora">Eldritch Energy Knight: Arcane Luminary of Eldora</span></h2>
<ol><li>Arcane Blade Mastery: Infuse your weapon with eldritch energy, empowering your attacks with arcane forces that can bypass defenses and disrupt magical barriers.</li>
<li>Eldritch Bolt: Unleash bolts of crackling energy infused with eldritch power, searing through enemies and leaving residual arcane energy that hinders their movements.</li>

<li>Arcane Echo: Manipulate residual arcane energy in the environment, causing it to resonate and create echoes of your own spells and abilities, effectively doubling their impact.</li>
<li>Eldritch Empowerment: Deepen your connection to the eldritch energies of Eldora, allowing you to tap into their limitless potential, exponentially increasing your arcane prowess for a limited time.</li>

<li>Eldritch Phase: Merge with the eldritch energies, phasing out of reality and becoming ethereal, rendering yourself immune to physical attacks and allowing you to pass through solid objects.</li>
<li>Arcane Luminary: Attain the pinnacle of mastery over eldritch energy, becoming a radiant beacon of arcane power, capable of weaving spells of immense destruction, summoning eldritch constructs, and harnessing the raw essence of Eldora's magic.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cosmic_Entity:_Nebuline_Shaper_of_Realms">Cosmic Entity: Nebuline Shaper of Realms</span></h2>

<li>Stellar Projection: Project your consciousness into the cosmos, traversing vast distances instantly, perceiving events across space and time, and gaining cosmic insights.</li>
<li>Celestial Embodiment: Embody the essence of celestial bodies, infusing yourself with their cosmic energies, granting enhanced attributes and cosmic abilities.</li>
<li>Astral Form: Transcend your physical form and assume a luminous astral manifestation, granting you ethereal qualities, heightened perception, and the ability to traverse astral planes.</li>
<li>Cosmic Resonance: Attune yourself to the cosmic vibrations, harmonizing with the celestial symphony, and gaining the ability to manipulate cosmic energy with precision and finesse.</li>
<li>Celestial Blessing: Call upon the blessings of the stars, channeling their radiant energies to bestow boons upon yourself and allies, enhancing their abilities and granting cosmic protection.</li>
<li>Nebuline Shaping: Shape the very nebulas themselves, fashioning them into weapons, shields, or cosmic constructs that aid you in battle, harnessing their immense power.</li>

<li>Celestial Ascendance: Ascend to a state of celestial transcendence, becoming a cosmic entity of immense power, capable of manipulating stars, creating supernovas, and altering the course of galaxies.</li>
<li>Nebuline Shaper of Realms: Attain the ultimate mastery over cosmic forces, becoming a living conduit of the nebulae, capable of shaping entire realms, manipulating cosmic energies on a grand scale, and transcending mortal limitations.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Narkum.27s_Prize:_Narkum.27s_Ascendant_Champion"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Narkum's_Prize:_Narkum's_Ascendant_Champion">Narkum's Prize: Narkum's Ascendant Champion</span></h2>
<ol><li>Narkum's Boon: Tap into the dark blessings bestowed by Narkum, empowering your abilities with unholy energy, increasing their potency and corrupting the life force of your enemies.</li>
<li>Void Embrace: Embrace the essence of the void, shrouding yourself in darkness, gaining increased stealth, agility, and the ability to sap the life force of those who come near.</li>
<li>Corrupting Touch: Infuse your attacks with the taint of Narkum's darkness, corrupting the life force of your enemies and draining their vitality with every strike.</li>
<li>Shadow Step: Traverse through shadows, teleporting instantly from one dark corner to another, allowing you to maneuver swiftly, surprise your foes, and strike with deadly precision.</li>
<li>Dark Pact: Form a pact with Narkum, granting you temporary bursts of dark power, enhancing your abilities, and enabling you to tap into forbidden knowledge and dark rituals.</li>
<li>Cursed Aura: Radiate a sinister aura that weakens and afflicts those who dare to approach you, draining their strength, sapping their vitality, and eroding their defenses.</li>
<li>Abyssal Whispers: Channel the whispers of Narkum, gaining insights into your enemies' weaknesses, revealing hidden information, and corrupting their thoughts and actions.</li>
<li>Void Veil: Shroud yourself in a veil of impenetrable darkness, rendering yourself nearly invisible to both the naked eye and magical detection, allowing for covert maneuvers and surprise attacks.</li>
<li>Narkum's Ascendance: Ascend to become an avatar of Narkum's dark power, manifesting as a nightmarish entity, exuding terror and wielding unparalleled control over the void, commanding its tendrils and consuming all who oppose you.</li>
<li>Narkum's Ascendant Champion: Claim the coveted title of Narkum's chosen champion, a living vessel of darkness and corruption, feared and revered by all who witness your malevolent power.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Energetic_Paladin:_Dynamo_Knight_of_Light">Energetic Paladin: Dynamo Knight of Light</span></h2>

<li>Sacred Smite: Consecrate your strikes with holy energy, imbuing them with explosive power that sears through evil, banishes dark entities, and leaves a lingering divine flame.</li>

<li>Energetic Resurgence: Tap into the boundless reserves of divine energy within yourself, rejuvenating your body and spirit, removing exhaustion, and granting temporary invigoration and heightened capabilities.</li>
<li>Luminous Transcendence: Ascend to a state of pure radiance, becoming a beacon of divine light, embodying the essence of righteousness and becoming a living embodiment of the divine's will.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Gem_Knight:_Crystalborne_Paragon">Gem Knight: Crystalborne Paragon</span></h2>
<ol><li>Crystal Blade Mastery: Wield your gem-infused blade with unparalleled skill and precision, striking with the crystalline essence that enhances your attacks, shattering defenses and leaving enemies vulnerable.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Gem_Mage:_Luminous_Gemweaver">Gem Mage: Luminous Gemweaver</span></h2>
<ol><li>Gemfire Bolt: Launch bolts of concentrated gemfire, projectiles imbued with the essence of gemstones, searing through enemies and leaving behind radiant trails of energy.</li>

<li>Gemweaving Mastery: Weave intricate patterns of gemstone magic, casting spells and creating complex magical formations that enhance your control over gemstone energy and amplify their effects.</li>
<li>Crystal Channeling: Establish a direct connection with the cosmic energies of gemstones, channeling their power through your body, enhancing your magical prowess and granting you heightened senses and perception.</li>
<li>Crystal Veil: Shroud yourself in a veil of refracting gemstone light, becoming ethereal and elusive, while empowering your spells and abilities with the luminous essence of gemstones.</li>
<li>Luminous Fusion: Merge your consciousness with the radiant energies of gemstones, transcending mortal limitations, and unlocking the full potential of gemstone magic, granting you mastery over light and radiant forces.</li>
<li>Prismatic Revelation: Unleash a radiant burst of multicolored energy, revealing hidden truths, dispelling illusions, and unmasking the true nature of enemies and their weaknesses.</li>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Parasitic_Gem_Conjurer:_Crystal_Devourer_Sorcerer">Parasitic Gem Conjurer: Crystal Devourer Sorcerer</span></h2>
<ol><li>Crystal Drain: Draw upon the life force of enemies and absorb it into gemstones, replenishing your own vitality and fueling your spells and abilities with stolen energy.</li>
<li>Gemstone Infestation: Infest targets with parasitic gemstones, causing them to suffer from debilitating effects, such as reduced defenses, weakened magic resistance, or slowed movement.</li>
<li>Crystal Devourer's Embrace: Assume a spectral form composed of swirling gemstone energy, granting you enhanced mobility, ethereal defenses, and the ability to pass through solid objects.</li>
<li>Gemstone Bursts: Release explosive bursts of raw gemstone energy, rupturing the earth and unleashing devastating shockwaves that damage enemies and disrupt their actions.</li>
<li>Crystal Corruption: Infect the environment with crystalline corruption, causing gemstones to grow uncontrollably, hindering enemy movement, and distorting their senses.</li>
<li>Crystal Leech: Extend tendrils of parasitic gemstone energy, draining the life force of enemies, sapping their strength, and transferring it to yourself or your allies.</li>
<li>Crystalized Enslavement: Imprison enemies within crystalline constructs, rendering them immobile and vulnerable to further attacks, while harnessing their trapped essence to empower your own magic.</li>
<li>Devourer's Resilience: Bolster your defenses with the energies of the crystals you consume, gaining increased resistance to damage, magical effects, and negative conditions.</li>
<li>Crystal Cascade: Unleash a cascading wave of crystal shards infused with parasitic energies, rending enemies' flesh and draining their life force, while restoring your own vitality.</li>

<h2><span id="Belial.27s_Chosen:_Belial.27s_Ascendant_Sentinel"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Belial's_Chosen:_Belial's_Ascendant_Sentinel">Belial's Chosen: Belial's Ascendant Sentinel</span></h2>

<li>Infernal Judgment: Invoke Belial's ultimate judgment upon the wicked, summoning forth a cataclysmic rain of hellfire that obliterates everything in its path, leaving only ashes and despair in its wake.</li></ol>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="One_of_Many_Faces:_Illusionist_Master_of_Shadows">One of Many Faces: Illusionist Master of Shadows</span></h2>
<ol><li>Shadowplay: Manipulate shadows to create illusions and conceal your true form, making it difficult for enemies to discern your identity and location.</li>
<li>Mirage Veil: Dissolve into a veil of shifting illusions, rendering yourself invisible and intangible, allowing you to pass through objects and evade enemy detection.</li>
<li>Illusory Decoy: Create convincing illusions of yourself or allies, distracting enemies and drawing their attention away from your true location.</li>
<li>Phantasmal Blades: Materialize ethereal blades of shadow, capable of inflicting both physical and psychological damage on your opponents, causing confusion and disorientation.</li>
<li>Illusionary Shroud: Envelop yourself in an ethereal shroud of illusions, rendering you impervious to direct attacks and granting you heightened resistance to magical and mental influences.</li>
<li>Veilwalker's Grace: Move with uncanny grace and agility, seamlessly blending into your surroundings and leaving behind afterimages that confuse and confound your enemies.</li>
<li>Illusionary Assault: Unleash a barrage of illusory attacks, overwhelming enemies with a flurry of phantom strikes that leave them vulnerable and disoriented.</li>
<li>Master of Deception: Hone your skills as an illusionist, deceiving even the most discerning eyes with your impeccable control over illusions, making it nearly impossible for enemies to distinguish reality from illusion.</li>
<li>Shadowmeld: Merge with the shadows, becoming one with the darkness itself, allowing you to move freely through the environment undetected and strike from unexpected angles.</li>
<li>Illusionist Master of Shadows: Ascend to the rank of Illusionist Master of Shadows, a master manipulator of perception and a true embodiment of the art of illusion, capable of crafting illusions so vivid and convincing that they become indistinguishable from reality.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Con_Artist:_Deception_Virtuoso">Con Artist: Deception Virtuoso</span></h2>
<ol><li>Silver Tongue: Possess a natural talent for persuasion and manipulation, able to convince others to see things your way or act in your favor through your charismatic words and gestures.</li>
<li>Illusory Fortune: Create illusions of wealth and opulence, convincing others of your prosperity and enticing them into your schemes with the promise of riches and rewards.</li>
<li>Master Disguise: Skillfully alter your appearance and assume different personas, allowing you to seamlessly blend into different social circles and deceive even the most vigilant observers.</li>
<li>Misdirection: Distract and divert attention away from your true intentions, using clever tactics and subtle gestures to lead others astray and create openings for your maneuvers.</li>
<li>Persuasive Ploy: Utilize psychological manipulation techniques to exploit the weaknesses and desires of others, bending them to your will and convincing them to act in ways they would not normally consider.</li>
<li>Art of Deception: Master the art of deception, honing your skills in sleight of hand, misdirection, and the artful use of props and illusions to create elaborate scams and cons.</li>
<li>Charismatic Aura: Radiate an aura of charm and charisma that draws others towards you, making it difficult for them to resist your influence or question your motives.</li>
<li>Mastermind's Gambit: Strategically plan and orchestrate complex schemes and heists, weaving a web of deception and manipulating events to ensure a favorable outcome for yourself and your allies.</li>
<li>Illusory Mirage: Create a grand illusion that envelops an entire area, distorting reality and disorienting all who enter, allowing you to carry out your schemes in the midst of chaos and confusion.</li>
<li>Deception Virtuoso: Rise to the pinnacle of your craft as a Deception Virtuoso, a master con artist capable of swaying the minds of even the most skeptical individuals and orchestrating elaborate deceptions on a grand scale.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Now_You_See_Me:_Elusive_Phantomwraith">Now You See Me: Elusive Phantomwraith</span></h2>
<ol><li>Vanishing Act: Dissolve into thin air, becoming completely invisible to the naked eye and eluding all forms of detection, allowing you to vanish from sight at will.</li>
<li>Shadow Step: Traverse great distances in the blink of an eye, moving effortlessly through the shadows and emerging from unexpected locations, catching your enemies off guard.</li>
<li>Illusory Afterimage: Leave behind a trail of illusory afterimages as you move, confusing enemies and making it difficult for them to track your true position or anticipate your next move.</li>
<li>Elusive Evasion: Possess extraordinary agility and reflexes, allowing you to effortlessly dodge incoming attacks and maneuver through even the most treacherous environments unscathed.</li>
<li>Phantasmal Assault: Launch swift and devastating strikes from the shadows, appearing and disappearing in an instant, leaving your enemies bewildered and vulnerable to your onslaught.</li>
<li>Illusionary Mirage: Project illusions of yourself in multiple locations simultaneously, creating a confusing array of phantoms that make it nearly impossible for enemies to discern your true location.</li>
<li>Spectral Cloak: Envelop yourself in a shroud of ethereal energy, rendering you intangible and immune to physical harm, passing through solid objects and evading enemy attacks with ease.</li>
<li>Illusionary Escape: Create a diversionary illusion that draws enemy attention away from you, allowing you to slip away unnoticed or set up advantageous positions for surprise attacks.</li>
<li>Phantom's Grasp: Manipulate the shadows to immobilize and bind your enemies, ensnaring them in a web of illusory tendrils that restrict their movements and leave them vulnerable to your subsequent strikes.</li>
<li>Elusive Phantomwraith: Ascend to the title of Elusive Phantomwraith, a master of stealth and illusion, capable of disappearing and reappearing at will, leaving behind only confusion and frustration in your wake.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Invisible:_Veilstorm_Vanisher">Invisible: Veilstorm Vanisher</span></h2>
<ol><li>Veil of Shadows: Shroud yourself in an impenetrable cloak of darkness, rendering yourself completely invisible to all forms of detection, including magical and supernatural means.</li>
<li>Silent Footsteps: Move with absolute silence and precision, leaving no trace or sound behind, enabling you to sneak past even the most vigilant guards and infiltrate heavily guarded areas undetected.</li>
<li>Illusionary Disguise: Bend light and manipulate perception, creating illusions that make you appear as someone or something else entirely, allowing you to seamlessly blend into your surroundings.</li>
<li>Phantom's Touch: Infuse your physical touch with ethereal energy, allowing you to pass through solid objects or phase through barriers, granting you access to otherwise inaccessible areas.</li>
<li>Shadowmeld: Merge with the shadows, becoming one with the darkness itself, making it nearly impossible for enemies to detect your presence or discern your true location.</li>
<li>Vanishing Strike: Launch a swift and devastating attack from the shadows, instantly disappearing after the strike, leaving your enemies bewildered and vulnerable to subsequent attacks.</li>
<li>Evasive Shadows: Manipulate the shadows around you to distort your silhouette and confuse enemy aim, causing projectiles and attacks to miss their mark.</li>
<li>Illusionary Distortion: Create illusions that distort the perception of space and distance, making it difficult for enemies to accurately gauge your position or launch effective attacks.</li>
<li>Shadowstep Mastery: Become a master of shadowstep techniques, allowing you to instantaneously teleport from one shadow to another, covering great distances and evading pursuit.</li>
<li>Veilstorm Vanisher: Attain mastery over the art of invisibility and shadow manipulation, earning the prestigious title of Veilstorm Vanisher, a ghostly apparition that can strike from the shadows and vanish without a trace.</li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cosmic_Entity:_Nebuline_Arbiter_of_Illusions">Cosmic Entity: Nebuline Arbiter of Illusions</span></h2>
<ol><li>Cosmic Illusion: Harness the power of cosmic energy to create illusions that transcend the boundaries of reality, manipulating the fabric of space and time to deceive and disorient your foes.</li>
<li>Astral Projection: Project your consciousness into the astral plane, allowing you to observe events from a distance and influence the perceptions of others through powerful illusions.</li>
<li>Stellar Veil: Surround yourself with a shimmering cosmic aura that distorts light and bends perception, making it difficult for enemies to accurately perceive your true form or intentions.</li>
<li>Nebuline Illusionist: Specialize in illusions inspired by the celestial bodies, conjuring visions of stars, galaxies, and nebulae that captivate and entrance your enemies, leaving them vulnerable to your manipulations.</li>
<li>Celestial Mirage: Create a celestial dreamscape, an ethereal realm where reality and illusion merge, overwhelming the senses of those who enter and distorting their perception of the world.</li>
<li>Cosmic Distortion: Twist and warp the laws of physics, creating illusions that defy logic and confound the minds of your enemies, causing them to question the nature of reality itself.</li>
<li>Illusory Transcendence: Transcend the limitations of physical form, assuming ephemeral and otherworldly manifestations that blur the line between illusion and reality, granting you enhanced powers of manipulation and deception.</li>
<li>Celestial Insight: Attain deep understanding of the cosmic forces at play, allowing you to tap into the underlying energies of the universe and channel them through your illusions, intensifying their effects and making them more convincing.</li>
<li>Astral Embrace: Merge with the astral plane, becoming one with the cosmic energies that permeate the universe, granting you heightened perception and a profound connection to the illusions you create.</li>
<li>Nebuline Arbiter of Illusions: Rise to the rank of Nebuline Arbiter of Illusions, a cosmic entity with unrivaled mastery over illusions, capable of weaving intricate webs of deception that span the vastness of the cosmos, influencing the perceptions and actions of both mortals and celestial beings alike.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Enon.27s_Forsaken:_Enon.27s_Haunting_Specter"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Enon's_Forsaken:_Enon's_Haunting_Specter">Enon's Forsaken: Enon's Haunting Specter</span></h2>
<ol><li>Haunting Presence: Exude an eerie and malevolent aura that instills fear and unease in those around you, making it difficult for them to maintain focus or muster the courage to stand against you.</li>
<li>Spectral Manifestation: Materialize as a haunting specter, your ethereal form flickering and shifting in and out of existence, allowing you to traverse between the mortal realm and the realm of spirits at will.</li>
<li>Enon's Curse: Unleash a cursed affliction upon your enemies, causing their senses to be distorted and their perception to be clouded by haunting visions and nightmarish illusions.</li>
<li>Wraithstep: Phase through solid objects and move unhindered through barriers, as if you were a ghostly apparition, allowing you to bypass physical obstacles and surprise your enemies from unexpected angles.</li>
<li>Soul Drain: Feed upon the life force of your enemies, siphoning their vitality and strength to empower yourself and weaken them, leaving them drained and vulnerable to your spectral assaults.</li>
<li>Enon's Echo: Summon spectral echoes of Enon's power, manifestations of his tormented spirit that aid you in battle, attacking enemies with otherworldly force and prolonging their suffering.</li>
<li>Ephemeral Evasion: Fade into the ethereal plane, becoming intangible and untouchable by physical means, rendering enemy attacks harmless as they pass harmlessly through your incorporeal form.</li>
<li>Soulweaver's Grasp: Reach into the depths of the spirit realm, grasping the essence of your enemies' souls and manipulating them to your advantage, ensnaring them in ethereal chains and rendering them helpless.</li>
<li>Enon's Embrace: Embrace the darkness and torment of Enon's forsaken existence, allowing his malevolent spirit to merge with yours, granting you heightened powers of illusion, manipulation, and haunting.</li>
<li>Enon's Haunting Specter: Ascend to the title of Enon's Haunting Specter, a harbinger of dread and despair, forever bound to the spirit of Enon and wielding his spectral powers to sow chaos and terror in the mortal realm.</li></ol>
<h2><span id="Varliri_Champion:_Varliri.27s_Ephemeral_Sentinel"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Varliri_Champion:_Varliri's_Ephemeral_Sentinel">Varliri Champion: Varliri's Ephemeral Sentinel</span></h2>
<ol><li>Ethereal Blade: Materialize a blade of pure energy, infused with the essence of Varliri, allowing you to strike with the power of the ephemeral realm, cutting through both physical and metaphysical barriers.</li>
<li>Echoing Steps: Step lightly and swiftly, leaving behind ethereal echoes that confuse enemies and distort their perception of your movements, granting you increased agility and evasiveness.</li>
<li>Spectral Armor: Summon a protective aura of ethereal energy that shields you from harm, reducing the effectiveness of enemy attacks and providing enhanced resistance to magical and physical damage.</li>
<li>Ephemeral Rift: Tear open a rift to the ephemeral realm, allowing spectral energies to surge forth and disrupt the flow of reality, weakening enemies and rendering their powers unstable.</li>
<li>Varliri's Embrace: Channel the ephemeral essence of Varliri, infusing yourself with its otherworldly power, enhancing your physical and magical abilities and imbuing your attacks with spectral energy.</li>
<li>Phantasmal Projection: Project illusions of yourself into multiple locations, confusing enemies and forcing them to divide their attention, creating openings for your attacks and maneuvers.</li>
<li>Sentinel's Resilience: Draw upon the resilience of the ephemeral realm, granting yourself enhanced endurance and the ability to withstand powerful blows and adverse conditions.</li>
<li>Ephemeral Manipulation: Manipulate the energies of the ephemeral realm to bend reality to your will, distorting perceptions, altering the flow of time, and influencing the outcomes of events in your favor.</li>
<li>Varliri's Ephemeral Bond: Forge a deep bond with Varliri, becoming its chosen champion and vessel, allowing you to channel its ethereal power with unparalleled mastery and serve as its emissary in the mortal realm.</li>
<li>Varliri's Ephemeral Sentinel: Embrace the title of Varliri's Ephemeral Sentinel, a guardian of the ephemeral realm and a formidable warrior, wielding the powers of the ethereal with unmatched skill and harnessing its energies to protect the balance between realms.</li></ol>
<h1><span class="mw-headline" id="Job_Expert">Job Expert</span></h1>
<pre>The Job Expert class represents characters who have mastered specific professions or occupations. They excel in their chosen field, possessing specialized skills, tools, or techniques that provide advantages in combat or offer unique utility to their team.
</pre>

<h1><span class="mw-headline" id="Archetypal_Specialization:">Archetypal Specialization:</span></h1>
<pre>The Archetypal Champion class embodies iconic archetypes and legends. They draw power and inspiration from legendary figures, mythical beings, or ancient traditions. These champions possess extraordinary abilities that set them apart from ordinary combatants.
</pre>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Fungal_Lord:_Guldaris_Visionary">The Fungal Lord: Guldaris Visionary</span></h2>
<p>the pinnacle of the fungus archetype, embodying mastery over a multitude of fungal variants. Within the skill module system, this class represents the highest level attainable in the realm of fungus manipulation. Let us delve into the abilities that define the Fungal Lord.
</p><p>Fungal Lance: Harness the power of conjuration, materializing a versatile fungal lance that can be imbued with various magical properties. Notably, the lance can be modified and upgraded without losing its enhancements, even if temporarily released from your grasp. The Fungal Lance boasts an extraordinary trait—a telekinetic bond that allows it to be summoned to your hand, regardless of its location. Furthermore, the lance possesses portable sentience, enabling the spreading of fungal networks upon contact with surfaces. This intricate network allows you to transfer your consciousness, seamlessly traversing between the fungal realms and the material plane, effectively materializing and dematerializing at will.
</p><p>Fungal Dominion: With an enraged countenance, unleash the full extent of your fungal power, ensnaring both friend and foe within a realm of fungal domination. However, be wary of its drawbacks. Upon activation, your humanoid form morphs into a fusion of fungus and mechanical parts, a bio-mechanical biomass. In this transformed state, constant vulnerability replaces your previous ability to defend yourself unfailingly. Throughout the duration, you endure periodic damage, representing the toll this form takes on your well-being. Should your health points plummet to zero while engulfed in this fungal dominion, you shall return to your original form but reduced to a mere spore, starting anew.
</p><p>Fungus Lord: Command the ability to instill fear and terror in the hearts of your enemies. Fungus Lord grants you the power to alter the very fabric of the environment, permeating it with an intricate network of fungal growth. This profound transformation extends beyond mere terrain alteration, resulting in permanent physical changes. Shedding all mechanical and bio-mechanical elements, your fungal form devours and assimilates their attributes, enhancing your own capabilities. Alongside the reshaped landscape, you gain the ability to manipulate the body composition of living beings, unveiling their true nature through the transformative prowess of the Fungal Lord.
</p><p>The Fungal Lord: Guldaris Visionary class stands as an embodiment of fungal supremacy, wielding these abilities to shape the course of battles and reshape the world around them.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Orderweaver:_Arbiter_of_Harmonious_Chaos">The Orderweaver: Arbiter of Harmonious Chaos</span></h2>
<p>The Orderweaver represents a unique archetype that brings forth the power of order, albeit in an unconventional manner. Rather than imposing permanent rules, the Orderweaver possesses the extraordinary ability to assign new regulations to preexisting concepts, thereby shaping the world's natural order, if only temporarily.
</p><p>Harmonious Edict: As an Orderweaver, you possess the remarkable skill of invoking the Harmonious Edict. With a mere gesture, you can establish a set of temporary rules, imbuing objects or entities with altered properties and behaviors. Through this ability, chaos can be harnessed and molded into a semblance of order, allowing you to create balance where none existed before. However, it is important to note that these alterations are not everlasting, and the world will eventually revert to its inherent state.
</p><p>Ethereal Scepter: A manifestation of your authority, the Ethereal Scepter is a conduit through which you channel your power. This mystic artifact grants you the ability to project the Harmonious Edict onto the world, reshaping its very fabric. With a mere touch of the scepter, you can assign new rules to the elements, creatures, or phenomena you encounter. From redirecting the flow of rivers to imbuing creatures with temporary abilities, the Ethereal Scepter is your tool to bring order to the chaos of existence.
</p><p>Transitory Mandate: The essence of your power lies in the transient nature of your mandates. Unlike traditional notions of order, the changes you instill are not permanent, but rather transient in nature. Your influence over the world's order is a delicate balance, offering a brief respite from the natural state of affairs. This transitory nature allows for adaptability and flexibility, as you can adjust the rules to suit the needs of any given situation.
</p><p>Eternal Paradox: As an Orderweaver, you are both a guardian and disruptor of the established order. The paradox lies in the duality of your role. You bring temporary structure to chaos, yet your actions are paradoxically imbued with an inherent unpredictability. It is within this delicate equilibrium that you wield your power, embracing the chaos while striving to bring about temporary harmony.
</p><p>The Orderweaver: Arbiter of Harmonious Chaos is a unique archetype that challenges conventional notions of order. Through the temporary assignment of new rules and the manipulation of existing concepts, you wield the power to create harmony in the ever-changing tapestry of existence.
</p>
